# 论语

<a id="ly-v01" name="ly-v01"></a>

## 学而篇第一

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-01)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_ef5866d11f0a.aspx)

<a id="ly-01-01" name="ly-01-01"></a>

### 1.1　学而时习之

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“学而时习之，不亦说乎？有朋自远方来，不亦乐乎？人不知而不愠，不亦君子乎？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，学到的道理能按时温习、亲自实践，心里自然喜悦。志同道合的朋友从远处来，怎会不快乐？别人不了解自己，也不因此怨恼，便有君子的修养。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">说，读 yuè，同“悦”。愠，心中怨恼。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三句话里，最后一种情形最见功夫：没有朋友来，甚至无人理解，还能否安然自处？</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-02" name="ly-01-02"></a>

### 1.2　其为人也孝弟

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有子曰：“其为人也孝弟，而好犯上者，鲜矣；不好犯上而好作乱者，未之有也。君子务本，本立而道生。孝弟也者，其为仁之本与！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有子说，一个人孝敬父母、尊敬兄长，却喜欢冒犯在上者，这种人很少。平素不爱犯上却喜欢作乱的，更没见过。君子把力气用在根本处，根本立稳，道才能生长。孝敬父母兄长，大概就是实践仁的根本吧。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">弟，读 tì，同“悌”，指敬爱兄长。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“本立而道生”取草木生长为喻，有子从家里能够练习的事说起。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-03" name="ly-01-03"></a>

### 1.3　巧言令色

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“巧言令色，鲜矣仁！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，话总拣讨喜的说，脸色总摆得叫人满意，这样的人少有真正的仁心。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">令色，特意摆出和悦的神色以取悦别人。鲜，少。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“鲜”留下了判断的分寸，也提醒人留意好听话背后的动机。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-04" name="ly-01-04"></a>

### 1.4　吾日三省吾身

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">曾子曰：“吾日三省吾身：为人谋而不忠乎？与朋友交而不信乎？传不习乎？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">曾子说，我每天反复检查自己。替人办事，有没有尽心？同朋友相处，有没有守信？老师传授的东西，有没有练习过？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">省，读 xǐng，反省、检查。传，这里指师长所授。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三问都落在可以回想的事情上，一天的言行就成了检点自己的凭据。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-05" name="ly-01-05"></a>

### 1.5　道千乘之国

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“道千乘之国，敬事而信，节用而爱人，使民以时。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，治理一个能出千辆兵车的国家，办事须尽心谨慎，承诺要可信。节约开支，爱惜人力，征用民力也要选择合宜的时候。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">使民以时，按合适的时节征发劳役，尤其要顾及农时。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从一次征役是否妨碍耕种，可以看出“爱人”怎样落实在政务里。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-06" name="ly-01-06"></a>

### 1.6　弟子入则孝

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“弟子入则孝，出则弟，谨而信，泛爱众，而亲仁，行有余力，则以学文。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，年轻人在家孝亲，出门敬长，办事谨慎，说话守信，广泛关爱众人，多亲近仁厚的人。做到这些还有余力，再学习诗书礼乐等文化。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">文，这里概指典籍、礼乐等文化之学。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">篇首说学习，这里把学习放回年轻人一天中会遇到的各种关系。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-07" name="ly-01-07"></a>

### 1.7　贤贤易色

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“贤贤易色；事父母，能竭其力；事君，能致其身；与朋友交，言而有信。虽曰未学，吾必谓之学矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，敬重贤德胜过看重美色，侍奉父母肯尽力，事奉君主肯献身，与朋友往来能够守信。即使这种人自称没有求过学，我也认定他已经学有所成。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">致其身，把自身交付出来，指肯尽力以至献身。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏验看一个人学得怎样，看的四件事都要在待人时显出来。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-08" name="ly-01-08"></a>

### 1.8　君子不重则不威

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子不重则不威，学则不固。主忠信，无友不如己者，过，则勿惮改。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子举止不庄重，就缺少使人敬重的威仪，所学也难以扎根。以尽心守信为主，不要同不如自己的人结交。发现过错，别害怕改正。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">惮，畏难。“学则不固”此处依所学不牢固之解。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">结交什么人、如何待自己的过错，这两件事都能影响所学是否稳固。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-09" name="ly-01-09"></a>

### 1.9　慎终追远

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">曾子曰：“慎终追远，民德归厚矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">曾子说，慎重地办理亲人的丧事，诚心追念久远的先人，百姓的风气也会逐渐趋于敦厚。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">终，这里指亲人的去世。远，指久远的祖先。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从亲人离世到追念远祖，亲厚之心如何延续，是这两句所关心的事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-10" name="ly-01-10"></a>

### 1.10　子禽问于子贡曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子禽问于子贡曰：“夫子至于是邦也，必闻其政，求之与，抑与之与？”子贡曰：“夫子温、良、恭、俭、让以得之。夫子之求之也，其诸异乎人之求之与？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子禽问子贡，孔子每到一国总能听到当地政务，是他去求教，还是人家主动相告？子贡说，老师以温和、善良、恭敬、节制、谦让的态度使人愿意相告。老师这种求知的方法，大概和别人有所不同吧。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">抑，还是，用来提出另一种可能。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子禽在问消息从哪里来，子贡请他看说话人为什么愿意开口。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-11" name="ly-01-11"></a>

### 1.11　父在

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“父在，观其志；父没，观其行；三年无改于父之道，可谓孝矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，父亲在世时，看一个人的志向。父亲去世后，看他的实际行为。三年间能保持父亲留下的行事之道，可以称得上孝了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">没，读 mò，去世。三年，联系古代为父母守丧的礼制。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“志”和“行”分别放在父亲在世与身后，观察的是儿子自己能做主时的选择。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-12" name="ly-01-12"></a>

### 1.12　礼之用

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有子曰：“礼之用，和为贵。先王之道，斯为美。小大由之，有所不行。知和而和，不以礼节之，亦不可行也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有子说，行礼贵在和谐，古代贤王的做法，美就美在这里。但大小事情一概只求和顺，也有行不通之处。光知道去求和，却没有礼来节制，也办不成事。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">节，用作动词，指调节分寸，使合乎尺度。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这段先称美“和”，再追问“和”靠什么维持分寸。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-13" name="ly-01-13"></a>

### 1.13　信近于义

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有子曰：“信近于义，言可复也。恭近于礼，远耻辱也。因不失其亲，亦可宗也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有子说，守信应当贴近义，作出的承诺才经得起兑现。恭敬应当合礼，才可远离耻辱。依靠的人若是值得亲近的，也就可以奉他为依归。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">复，这里指兑现诺言。宗，尊奉、依归。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有子把功夫推到答应之前：能不能兑现，要先问这件事合不合宜。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-14" name="ly-01-14"></a>

### 1.14　君子食无求饱

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子食无求饱，居无求安，敏于事而慎于言，就有道而正焉，可谓好学也已。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子吃饭不一味求饱足，居住不一味求舒适。做事勤快，说话慎重，还主动接近有道的人，请他纠正自己。这样就可称作好学。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">就，走近、前往。正，匡正自己。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">最后一步是请别人指出偏差，好学也要能给纠正留一个位置。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-15" name="ly-01-15"></a>

### 1.15　贫而无谄

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡曰：“贫而无谄，富而无骄，何如？”子曰：“可也。未若贫而乐，富而好礼者也。”子贡曰：“《诗》云：‘如切如磋，如琢如磨’，其斯之谓与？”子曰：“赐也，始可与言《诗》已矣，告诸往而知来者。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡问，贫穷却不奉承，富有却不傲慢，这样如何？孔子说，可以了，还赶不上贫而能乐、富而好礼。子贡想起《诗经》写工匠反复切磋琢磨，问是不是这个意思。孔子高兴地说，赐啊，现在可以同你谈《诗》了，告诉你一件事，你便能推出下一层。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">切、磋、琢、磨，是加工骨、角、玉、石等材料的工序。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子说了两层境界，子贡能再添一个工匠的比喻，正是“知来”的现场例子。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-01-16" name="ly-01-16"></a>

### 1.16　不患人之不己知

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“不患人之不己知，患不知人也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，不必为别人不了解自己忧愁，应当担心的是自己还看不清别人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">不己知，即“不知己”，不了解自己。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一个“知”字，调换谁是认识者，忧虑就变成了可以用功的事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-01)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v02" name="ly-v02"></a>

## 为政篇第二

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-02)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_eec1d4c6eb80.aspx)

<a id="ly-02-01" name="ly-02-01"></a>

### 2.1　为政以德

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“为政以德，譬如北辰，居其所而众星共之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，执政者靠德行来治理，像北极星安居其位，众星环绕着它。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">北辰，古人用来标示北天极的星。共，通“拱”，环绕。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这个比喻让人先看领导者立身的位置，再看众人怎样向他聚拢。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-02" name="ly-02-02"></a>

### 2.2　诗》三百

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“《诗》三百，一言以蔽之，曰：‘思无邪’。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，《诗经》三百篇的旨趣，用一句话可以概括，就是“思无邪”。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">蔽，概括、总括。思无邪，语出《诗经·鲁颂·駉》，孔子借来总评《诗》。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子借用《诗》中的一句来谈整部《诗》，读诗的方法也藏在这次引用中。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-03" name="ly-02-03"></a>

### 2.3　道之以政

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“道之以政，齐之以刑，民免而无耻。道之以德，齐之以礼，有耻且格。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，用政令引导、用刑罚整齐百姓，人们可能只求免罚，心中没有羞耻感。以德行引导、以礼节约束，则人能知耻，也会自己归向正道。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">格，此处依“正、归正”之解。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两种治理都可能使人守规矩，孔子还要问，当惩罚不在眼前时人会怎么做。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-04" name="ly-02-04"></a>

### 2.4　吾十有五而志于学

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“吾十有五而志于学，三十而立，四十而不惑，五十而知天命，六十而耳顺，七十而从心所欲，不逾矩。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子回顾自己：十五岁立志求学，三十岁有了立身的根基，四十岁遇事不易迷惑，五十岁懂得天命，六十岁听人说话能够通达，七十岁依心意行事也不会越过应守的尺度。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">矩，本是画方形的尺，引申为应守的规度。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从“志于学”到“从心所欲”，准则逐步化入了自己的判断和举止。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-05" name="ly-02-05"></a>

### 2.5　孟懿子问孝

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孟懿子问孝，子曰：“无违。”樊迟御，子告之曰：“孟孙问孝于我，我对曰‘无违’。”樊迟曰：“何谓也？”子曰：“生，事之以礼；死，葬之以礼，祭之以礼。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孟懿子请问孝，孔子答，不可违背。后来樊迟驾车，孔子提起这句话，樊迟追问不能违背什么。孔子说明，父母在世，依礼奉养。去世以后，依礼安葬，也依礼祭祀。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">御，驾车。无违，据下文是说不违背礼。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“无违”的对象要读到樊迟发问才明白，后半段也替读者问了一次。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-06" name="ly-02-06"></a>

### 2.6　孟武伯问孝

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孟武伯问孝。子曰：“父母唯其疾之忧。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孟武伯问孝。孔子的回答是，让父母只为你的疾病担忧。意思是，子女应谨慎自持，少让父母为自己的行为操心。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">其，此处解为子女。此句也有从体会父母忧子之心来释孝的读法。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">回答只举父母的一种忧虑，却让人想到生活中还有多少忧虑原可避免。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-07" name="ly-02-07"></a>

### 2.7　子游问孝

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子游问孝。子曰：“今之孝者，是谓能养。至于犬马，皆能有养；不敬，何以别乎？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子游问孝。孔子说，如今有人以为供得起父母就叫孝，然而犬马也能得到饲养。若缺少敬意，把人奉养起来和养动物如何分别？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">养，供给饮食生活所需。“犬马皆能有养”这里依犬马亦受饲养之解。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“敬”字放在物质供养之后，点出了单凭账目看不见的部分。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-08" name="ly-02-08"></a>

### 2.8　子夏问孝

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏问孝。子曰：“色难。有事，弟子服其劳；有酒食，先生馔，曾是以为孝乎？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏问孝。孔子说，难的是脸上的神色。遇事晚辈代劳，有酒饭请长辈先用，只做到这些，难道就算孝了吗？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">色，脸色、神情。先生，指父兄等先于自己出生的人。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">端饭、服劳都做得到，做这些事时脸上的神色仍值得细看。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-09" name="ly-02-09"></a>

### 2.9　吾与回言终日

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“吾与回言终日，不违，如愚。退而省其私，亦足以发，回也不愚。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，我同颜回谈了一整天，他没有一句反对，像是愚钝。我观察他离开以后私下的言行，却见他足以发挥所听的道理。颜回并不愚钝。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">回，颜回。发，把领会的意思表现出来、加以发挥。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子把观察延长到谈话结束以后，才看清这位沉默的学生。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-10" name="ly-02-10"></a>

### 2.10　视其所以

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“视其所以，观其所由，察其所安，人焉廋哉？人焉廋哉？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，看他做什么，看他走什么路，再看他心里安于什么。这样看，一个人还能藏到哪里去？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">廋，读 sōu，隐藏。安，内心安于、乐于。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三个动词把观察由行动推向来路，最后推到一个人的安顿之处。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-11" name="ly-02-11"></a>

### 2.11　温故而知新

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“温故而知新，可以为师矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，反复温习已经学过的东西，又能由此生出新的认识，就可以做老师了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">故，已经学到、知道的内容。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">旧材料能不能回答新问题，是这句话对“温习”的要求。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-12" name="ly-02-12"></a>

### 2.12　君子不器

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子不器。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子不应把自己局限为一件只有固定用途的器具。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">器，器具，各有一定用途。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">许多场合都要自己作判断，一项熟练的本领还不能替人作尽所有选择。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-13" name="ly-02-13"></a>

### 2.13　子贡问君子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡问君子。子曰：“先行其言而后从之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡问怎样做君子。孔子说，要讲的事先做出来，再让话跟在行动后面。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">从，跟随，此处说言语跟随行动。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这句话只调整言与行的先后，却能检验一句许诺有多少分量。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-14" name="ly-02-14"></a>

### 2.14　君子周而不比

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子周而不比，小人比而不周。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子周到地对待众人，不结成偏私的小圈子。小人为了私利结党，不能公平地照顾众人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">周，广泛而公正地与人相处。比，读 bì，此处指私相亲附。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两种人都在与人来往，分别在亲近一方会不会因此亏待另一方。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-15" name="ly-02-15"></a>

### 2.15　学而不思则罔

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“学而不思则罔，思而不学则殆。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，只管学而不思考，容易迷惘而无所得。只顾自己思考却不肯求学，认识也会陷入危险。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">罔，迷惘。殆，这里取有危险、靠不住之意。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">学习给思考以凭据，思考也让人辨清所学到底说了什么。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-16" name="ly-02-16"></a>

### 2.16　攻乎异端

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“攻乎异端，斯害也已！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，专力钻研偏离正道的说法，会造成祸害。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">攻，这里取用力研习之意。“异端”的确切所指历来有不同解释。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这一句没有列出具体学派，解释时应把孔子的话和后人划定的派别界线分开。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-17" name="ly-02-17"></a>

### 2.17　由

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“由，诲女知之乎！知之为知之，不知为不知，是知也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子对仲由说，我来教你怎样对待知道与不知道。清楚的承认清楚，还没弄懂的就承认没弄懂，这才是真正明智。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">女，通“汝”，你。末句的“知”可读为“智”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">最后一个“知”问的是能否准确辨认前面两种状态。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-18" name="ly-02-18"></a>

### 2.18　子张学干禄

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张学干禄。子曰：“多闻阙疑，慎言其余，则寡尤；多见阙殆，慎行其余，则寡悔。言寡尤，行寡悔，禄在其中矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张想学谋求俸禄的办法。孔子说，多听，有疑问的先放一放，对其余的也慎重开口，便少过失。多看，没把握的先放一放，对其余的也谨慎去做，便少后悔。言行做到了这两点，求仕的条件也就在其中。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">干禄，求取俸禄。阙，暂且搁置。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">多闻多见以后仍有该留白的地方，“阙”字给言行留下了核实的时间。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-19" name="ly-02-19"></a>

### 2.19　哀公问曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">哀公问曰：“何为则民服？”孔子对曰：“举直错诸枉，则民服；举枉错诸直，则民不服。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">鲁哀公问，怎样才能使百姓信服？孔子答，把正直的人提拔起来，放在邪曲者之上，百姓就服。若把邪曲的人压在正直者头上，百姓便不服。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">错，这里同“措”，安置。直、枉分别借指正直和邪曲的人。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">君主一次用人的决定，就向百姓表明谁会受到奖赏。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-20" name="ly-02-20"></a>

### 2.20　季康子问

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">季康子问：“使民敬、忠以劝，如之何？”子曰：“临之以庄，则敬；孝慈，则忠；举善而教不能，则劝。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">季康子问怎样让百姓恭敬、尽心并愿意努力。孔子答，庄重地对待他们，人们便会恭敬。自己孝亲慈幼，人们便会尽心。提拔有长处的人，教导还不会的人，大家就能受到鼓励。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">劝，在这里指受到勉励、肯努力。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">季康子问要百姓怎样，孔子三次从主政者能先做什么回答。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-21" name="ly-02-21"></a>

### 2.21　或谓孔子曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">或谓孔子曰：“子奚不为政？”子曰：“《书》云：‘孝乎惟孝，友于兄弟，施于有政。’是亦为政，奚其为为政？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有人问孔子为什么不参与政事。孔子引《尚书》的话说，孝顺父母，友爱兄弟，这份行为影响到政事，本已是在参与治理。何必非得有官职才算从政？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">友于兄弟，对兄弟友爱。施，延及、影响。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子把家庭中能够亲自实行的责任也纳入了政治生活。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-22" name="ly-02-22"></a>

### 2.22　人而无信

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“人而无信，不知其可也。大车无輗，小车无軏，其何以行之哉？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，一个人没有信用，实在不知道他凭什么行得通。好比大车少了輗，小车少了軏，还怎么走得起来？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">輗（ní）、軏（yuè），古代车辕前端连接牵引装置的部件。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">信用像一枚连接部件，本身不显眼，一缺少它，原本齐备的车子也走不了。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-23" name="ly-02-23"></a>

### 2.23　子张问

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张问：“十世可知也？”子曰：“殷因于夏礼，所损益，可知也；周因于殷礼，所损益，可知也。其或继周者，虽百世，可知也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张问，往后十代的情况能知道吗？孔子答，商继承夏礼，有哪些增减可以考见。周继承商礼，增减也有迹可寻。若有继周而起的朝代，依这种承袭变通的道理，百代之后也能推知个大概。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">因，沿袭。损益，减去与增加。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子给的线索是可考察的制度变迁，推论将来先得看清以前改动了什么。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-02-24" name="ly-02-24"></a>

### 2.24　非其鬼而祭之

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“非其鬼而祭之，谄也；见义不为，无勇也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，祭祀不属于自己应祭的鬼神，是有意求媚。看见义所当为的事却不去做，是缺少勇气。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">鬼，这里指应依身份、亲属关系祭祀的亡灵。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一件是越过自己的本分讨好，一件是遇到自己的本分却后退。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-02)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v03" name="ly-v03"></a>

## 八佾篇第三

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-03)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_7c068bb8a890.aspx)

<a id="ly-03-01" name="ly-03-01"></a>

### 3.1　孔子谓季氏

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子谓季氏：“八佾舞于庭，是可忍也，孰不可忍也？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">季氏在自家庭院里用天子规格的八佾舞。孔子评论，这种事他都忍心做，还有什么做不出来？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">佾，读 yì，古代舞队的行列。八佾是天子的礼制规格。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">读懂“于庭”便知道孔子为何动怒：表演地点是大夫的私家庭院。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-02" name="ly-03-02"></a>

### 3.2　三家者以《雍》彻

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三家者以《雍》彻，子曰：“‘相维辟公，天子穆穆’，奚取于三家之堂？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">鲁国三家大夫祭祀结束时唱《雍》诗撤去祭品。孔子问，诗唱的是诸侯助祭、天子仪态庄严，三家厅堂里哪一点当得起这样的歌词？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">彻，通“撤”，撤下祭品。辟公，诸侯。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子的批评直接取自歌词：堂上实际的人，能与歌里的人对得上吗？</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-03" name="ly-03-03"></a>

### 3.3　人而不仁

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“人而不仁，如礼何？人而不仁，如乐何？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，人没有仁心，该怎样实行礼？人没有仁心，又怎么能真正运用乐？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">乐，这里指与礼制相连的音乐、歌舞。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两次问“如何”，让目光从器物和仪节移到主持仪式的人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-04" name="ly-03-04"></a>

### 3.4　林放问礼之本

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">林放问礼之本，子曰：“大哉问！礼，与其奢也，宁俭；丧，与其易也，宁戚。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">林放问礼的根本。孔子称赞这个问题重要：一般礼节与其奢华，宁可俭朴。办理丧事，与其仪节办得娴熟周全，宁愿哀伤真切。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">易，此处取治办熟练、仪节周到之意。戚，悲哀。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">丧礼办得流畅与心里悲哀是两件事，林放问“本”，孔子便在两者难兼时作选择。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-05" name="ly-03-05"></a>

### 3.5　夷狄之有君

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“夷狄之有君，不如诸夏之亡也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子感叹，连当时被称作夷狄的地方尚且有君长，诸夏的君臣名分却乱得像没有君主了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">亡，通“无”。“不如”也有解作“不及”者，读作夷狄虽有君仍不及诸夏无君。此处从讥诸夏失序之解。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这一句历来有不同读法，联系篇中鲁国大夫僭礼的事，可以读出对当时政治的感慨。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-06" name="ly-03-06"></a>

### 3.6　季氏旅于泰山

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">季氏旅于泰山。子谓冉有曰：“女弗能救与？”对曰：“不能。”子曰：“呜呼！曾谓泰山不如林放乎？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">季氏越过身份去祭泰山。孔子问在季氏任职的冉有，不能设法劝阻吗？冉有说不能。孔子叹息，难道泰山之神辨别礼的能力，还不如曾来请教礼之本的林放？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">旅，按礼举行的山川祭祀。救，在这里指制止失礼之举。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">上文问礼的林放在此再度出现，孔子借他反问，祭山的人有没有先问自己是否合礼。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-07" name="ly-03-07"></a>

### 3.7　君子无所争

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子无所争，必也射乎！揖让而升，下而饮。其争也君子。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子平素没有什么要同人争。若说有，大概是射礼吧。上场前作揖谦让，下场后依礼饮酒，连这种竞争也能显出君子的风度。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">揖，两手合拱行礼。饮，指射礼结束后的饮酒礼节。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有了胜负，仍要一同完成前后的礼节，才看得出“君子之争”。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-08" name="ly-03-08"></a>

### 3.8　子夏问曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏问曰：“‘巧笑倩兮，美目盼兮，素以为绚兮’何谓也？”子曰：“绘事后素。”曰：“礼后乎？”子曰：“起予者商也，始可与言《诗》已矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏问，诗写女子笑得好看、眼睛顾盼有神，又以素白衬出绚丽，是什么意思？孔子答，绘画要有素地作根基。子夏便问，礼也要后于内在的质地吗？孔子赞叹，是商启发了我，现在可以同你谈《诗》了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">商，子夏之名。“绘事后素”有不同解说，此处取先有素地而后施彩。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">学生从绘画想到礼，老师也承认受了启发，这场问答本身就值得留意。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-09" name="ly-03-09"></a>

### 3.9　夏礼吾能言之

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“夏礼吾能言之，杞不足征也；殷礼吾能言之，宋不足征也。文献不足故也，足则吾能征之矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，夏代的礼我能讲，但杞国留存的东西不够用来验证。殷礼我也能讲，宋国的依据同样不足。典籍和通晓旧礼的人不够，若材料充足，我就能加以验证。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">征，验证。文献，古注以“文”为典籍、“献”为贤者。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">能说出一套旧礼与能拿材料证实它，孔子分得很清楚。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-10" name="ly-03-10"></a>

### 3.10　禘自既灌而往者

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“禘自既灌而往者，吾不欲观之矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，举行禘祭，开头酌酒灌地的礼节过后，我就不想再看下去了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">禘，读 dì，宗庙中的大祭。灌，以酒灌地的祭仪。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子明确说从什么时候起不想看，却没有在此章指明是哪一道程序出了问题。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-11" name="ly-03-11"></a>

### 3.11　或问禘之说

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">或问禘之说。子曰：“不知也。知其说者之于天下也，其如示诸斯乎！”指其掌。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有人问禘祭的道理。孔子说，我不知道。真懂这一道理的人面对治理天下，大约像看这里一样清楚吧。说着指了指自己的手掌。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">示诸斯，这里指给人看眼前的这个地方，所指由下文的手掌点明。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句“不知”之后，孔子没有讲繁复仪节，反用手掌打了一个治理天下的比方。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-12" name="ly-03-12"></a>

### 3.12　祭如在

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">祭如在，祭神如神在。子曰：“吾不与祭，如不祭。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">祭祀祖先，像祖先真在眼前。祭神，也像神就在面前。孔子说，应当由我参加的祭祀，若我没有亲自参与，对我便等于没祭。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">与，读 yù，参加。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两个“如在”要求祭者在心中面对所祭者，末句进一步问祭者自己在不在场。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-13" name="ly-03-13"></a>

### 3.13　王孙贾问曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">王孙贾问曰：“‘与其媚于奥，宁媚于灶’，何谓也？”子曰：“不然，获罪于天，无所祷也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">王孙贾引用俗语，说与其讨好屋里的尊位，不如讨好灶神，问这是什么意思。孔子答，不是这样。若已经得罪了天，到哪里祷求都没用了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">奥，室内西南角，是室中尊位。灶，烹饪处，此处借指灶神。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">俗语问向谁求更灵，孔子把话题移到了求告者自己做了什么。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-14" name="ly-03-14"></a>

### 3.14　周监于二代

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“周监于二代，郁郁乎文哉！吾从周。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，周代参考夏商两代礼制，形成了何等丰富完备的文化！我愿意遵从周礼。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">监，参照、借鉴。二代，夏和商。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">在“从周”之前先说周怎样借鉴前人，交代了选择所凭的理由。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-15" name="ly-03-15"></a>

### 3.15　子入太庙

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子入太庙，每事问。或曰：“孰谓鄹人之子知礼乎？入太庙，每事问。”子闻之，曰：“是礼也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子进鲁国太庙，每件事都询问。有人因此讥笑，谁说这位鄹人的儿子懂礼，怎么事事还要问？孔子听见了说，进庙这样询问，本来就是礼。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">鄹，读 zōu，鲁地名。鄹人之子，指孔子。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">旁人把提问当作不懂，孔子却把询问本身也算进了礼节。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-16" name="ly-03-16"></a>

### 3.16　射不主皮

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“射不主皮，为力不同科，古之道也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，按古时的射礼，评射不以能否射穿皮革为主要标准，因为人们的力气并不相同。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">皮，指箭靶所用的皮革。科，等类。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">既然射手的力量各异，评射的规矩就不能只看穿透的深浅。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-17" name="ly-03-17"></a>

### 3.17　子贡欲去告朔之饩羊

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡欲去告朔之饩羊，子曰：“赐也！尔爱其羊，我爱其礼。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡想免掉每月告朔仪式所供的活羊。孔子说，赐啊，你爱惜的是这只羊，我爱惜的是这一礼仪。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">告朔，古代在月初举行的典礼。饩羊，供礼所用的活羊。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">师生的“爱”都有所指，同一只羊牵涉了眼前耗费与礼制延续两层问题。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-03-18" name="ly-03-18"></a>

### 3.18　事君尽礼

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“事君尽礼，人以为谄也。”</font></p>
