<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">苗、花、果三个阶段，后一处都不会仅因前一处已经有了就自动实现。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-23" name="ly-09-23"></a>

### 9.23　后生可畏

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“后生可畏，焉知来者之不如今也？四十、五十而无闻焉，斯亦不足畏也已。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">年轻的后辈值得敬重，谁能断定后来的赶不上现在的人？然而到了四五十岁还没什么值得称道的成就，也就不足以使人敬畏了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">畏，此处有敬畏、重视的意思。闻，声名、为人所知。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">前半为后人保留可能，后半问人是否把年月用出了结果。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-24" name="ly-09-24"></a>

### 9.24　法语之言

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“法语之言，能无从乎？改之为贵。巽与之言，能无说乎？绎之为贵。说而不绎，从而不改，吾末如之何也已矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">合乎法度的正面劝告，谁会不表示听从？可贵的是肯改。和婉的意见，谁会不乐于听？可贵的是仔细寻绎。只爱听而不推想，口称听从而不改变，我也拿这样的人没办法。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">巽与，谦顺婉转。绎，循着意思细加推究。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">对直言和婉言，孔子安排了不同的下一步：一种贵改，一种贵绎。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-25" name="ly-09-25"></a>

### 9.25　主忠信

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“主忠信。毋友不如己者，过，则勿惮改。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">把忠信作为立身的根本，交朋友要能从对方的品行中得到长进，犯了过错便别害怕改正。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">惮，害怕。“毋友不如己者”常解为不要同德行不及自己的人交友。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">选朋友和改过紧接着出现，关注的都是守住并改进自己的品行。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-26" name="ly-09-26"></a>

### 9.26　三军可夺帅也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“三军可夺帅也，匹夫不可夺志也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">大军的统帅可以被夺去，普通人自己的志向却无法从他心里强行夺走。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">匹夫，普通人。夺，强行取去。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">将帅可以被更换，志向归根到底要由本人决定是否守住。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-27" name="ly-09-27"></a>

### 9.27　衣敝缊袍

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“衣敝缊袍，与衣狐貉者立而不耻者，其由也与！‘不忮不求，何用不臧？’”子路终身诵之，子曰：“是道也，何足以臧？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">穿着破旧丝絮袍，同穿狐貉皮衣的人并肩站着也不羞惭，大概就是仲由吧。孔子引诗赞他，不嫉妒、不贪求，有什么不好？子路一直诵这两句，孔子又提醒，只守着这一条，哪里足以尽善？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">忮（zhì），嫉害。臧，善。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一位老师先肯定学生，随后又怕他把值得肯定的一点当成终点。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-28" name="ly-09-28"></a>

### 9.28　岁寒

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“岁寒，然后知松柏之后凋也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">等到一年中最寒冷的时候，才看得出松柏比别的树木晚凋。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">后凋，凋落较晚。岁寒，时序入寒。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">共同经历的严寒，使先前不显眼的一种差别变得可见。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-29" name="ly-09-29"></a>

### 9.29　知者不惑

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“知者不惑，仁者不忧，勇者不惧。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">智慧使人不迷惑，仁厚使人不忧惧，勇敢使人不畏缩。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">知，通“智”。惑，疑迷、无所判断。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三种德行各自回答一个难处，把疑惑、忧虑、畏惧分开了。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-30" name="ly-09-30"></a>

### 9.30　可与共学

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“可与共学，未可与适道；可与适道，未可与立；可与立，未可与权。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">可以跟一个人共同求学，未必已经能同他走向正道。即便同行，也未必能一起立得稳。能立得稳的人，还未必能一道权衡复杂事情的轻重。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">适，往、趋向。权，因事衡量轻重。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">每一层开头接住上一层的成就，又留下一个更难的“未可”。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-09-31" name="ly-09-31"></a>

### 9.31　唐棣之华，偏其反而。岂不尔

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“唐棣之华，偏其反而。岂不尔思？室是远尔。”子曰：“未之思也，夫何远之有。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">诗句说，唐棣花摇动，哪里是不想你，只是你的住处太远。孔子说，是还没有真正想念吧，若真想念，又有什么远呢？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">唐棣，植物名。偏其反而，古注多释作花枝摇曳。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句诗把阻碍归在路远，孔子把同一个“远”字放回说话人的心意里追问。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-09)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v10" name="ly-v10"></a>

## 乡党篇第十

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-10)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_c9510cb3189a.aspx)

<a id="ly-10-01" name="ly-10-01"></a>

### 10.1　孔子于乡党

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子于乡党，恂恂如也，似不能言者；其在宗庙朝庭，便便言，唯谨尔。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子在家乡待人温顺诚恳，好像不善言辞。在宗庙和朝廷上，他把话说得清楚流畅，同时处处谨慎。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">恂恂，恭顺诚恳的样子。便便，言辞明白顺畅。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两种说话方式放在乡里和公事两个场合，变化中仍看得出一贯的分寸。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-02" name="ly-10-02"></a>

### 10.2　朝

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">朝，与下大夫言，侃侃如也；与上大夫言，訚訚如也。君在，踧踖如也，与与如也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">在朝堂同下大夫讲话，他从容坦率。同上大夫讲话，态度和悦庄重。君主在场，他显得恭敬谨慎，又举止合度。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">訚訚（yín yín），和悦而能正言。踧踖（cù jí），恭谨的样子。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">记录者用几对相近而有差别的叠字，把几种相处的情形分开。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-03" name="ly-10-03"></a>

### 10.3　君召使摈

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">君召使摈，色勃如也，足躩如也。揖所与立，左右手，衣前后，襜如也。趋进，翼如也。宾退，必复命曰：“宾不顾矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">君主召他协助接待宾客，他神色郑重，脚步加快。向左右同列者行揖，衣服前后整齐地摆动。快步上前，姿态舒展如翼。客人退去，他一定返回报告，说明客人已走远，不再回望。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">摈，协助主人接待宾客。复命，办完交还使命、报告结果。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一项迎宾事务何时才算结束，要等到客人确已远去，主人也得到回报。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-04" name="ly-10-04"></a>

### 10.4　入公门

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">入公门，鞠躬如也，如不容。立不中门，行不履阈。过位，色勃如也，足躩如也，其言似不足者。摄齐升堂，鞠躬如也，屏气似不息者。出，降一等，逞颜色，怡怡如也；没阶，趋进，翼如也；复其位，踧踖如也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">进入公门，他低身恭敬，像是门窄容不下自己。站立不占门中，走路不踏门槛。经过君位，神色和脚步都谨慎起来，说话也放轻。提起衣摆上堂时，躬身屏息。退下一级台阶后，脸色舒缓，走完台阶快步如展翼，回到本位又恢复恭谨。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">阈，门槛。摄齐（zī），提起衣裳下摆。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一次往返里，叙述跟着脚步改变神色，连舒缓从哪一级台阶开始都记下了。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-05" name="ly-10-05"></a>

### 10.5　执圭

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">执圭，鞠躬如也，如不胜。上如揖，下如授。勃如战色，足蹜蹜如有循。享礼，有容色。私觌，愉愉如也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">出使执圭，孔子躬着身，像是担不起它的分量。举圭像作揖，放低像交付，神色庄严，步子细密，好像沿线而走。到行献礼时面容和缓，私下会见时就更加愉快。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">圭，出使所执的玉器。私觌（dí），正式礼仪之后的私人会见。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一趟出使有不同环节，脸色的变化和礼仪的进展彼此配合。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-06" name="ly-10-06"></a>

### 10.6　君子不以绀緅饰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">君子不以绀緅饰，红紫不以为亵服。当暑袗絺绤，必表而出之。缁衣羔裘，素衣麑裘，黄衣狐裘。亵裘长，短右袂。必有寝衣，长一身有半。狐貉之厚以居。去丧，无所不佩。非帷裳，必杀之。羔裘玄冠不以吊。吉月，必朝服而朝。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">衣缘不用绀、緅两色，家居便服也不用红、紫。暑天穿单层葛布衣，出门仍加外衣。黑衣配黑羊羔皮，白衣配小鹿皮，黄衣配狐皮。家居皮袍长而右袖短，另有一身半长的寝衣，闲居用厚狐貉皮。除丧后恢复佩饰，除礼服直幅的下裳外，衣裳按形裁制。不穿羔裘戴黑礼帽去吊丧，每月初一必着朝服上朝。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">绀（gàn）、緅（zōu），两种深色。杀，此处指裁削衣料。吉月，月初朔日。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">长袍特意缩短右袖，是为了动作方便，衣制的记录里也有日常身体的需要。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-07" name="ly-10-07"></a>

### 10.7　齐

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">齐，必有明衣，布。齐必变食，居必迁坐。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">斋戒时一定预备洁净的布衣，还要改换平时的饮食，变更平日坐卧之处。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">明衣，斋戒时所用的洁净衣服。齐，通“斋”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">斋戒从衣、食、居三处开始，把身体习惯一一调整。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-08" name="ly-10-08"></a>

### 10.8　食不厌精

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">食不厌精，脍不厌细。食饐而餲，鱼馁而肉败，不食；色恶，不食；臭恶，不食；失饪，不食；不时，不食；割不正，不食；不得其酱，不食。肉虽多，不使胜食气。唯酒无量，不及乱。沽酒市脯，不食。不撤姜食，不多食。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">米精、肉切得细，都不嫌讲究。饭馊了，鱼肉变质，颜色或气味不对，烹调失当，不合时宜，切割不合规矩，配酱不合适，都不吃。即使肉多，分量也不盖过主食。饮酒不定一律的数目，限度是神志行为不乱。外买的酒和肉干不用，桌上留姜，进食不贪多。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">脍，切细的肉。餲（ài），食物变味。脯，肉干。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">诸多“不食”之后仍提到酒无定量，这份饮食记录既看食物，也看进食者是否守得住分寸。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-09" name="ly-10-09"></a>

### 10.9　祭于公

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">祭于公，不宿肉。祭肉不出三日，出三日不食之矣。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">参加公家的祭祀，分得的祭肉不留到次日。自家祭祀的肉也不放过三天，超过三天就不再食用。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">宿，过夜。祭于公，参与国君主持的祭祀。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">文中分开公祭与家祭，同是祭肉，各有对待的时限。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-10" name="ly-10-10"></a>

### 10.10　食不语

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">食不语，寝不言。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">吃饭时不交谈，上床就寝后不说话。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">语，交谈。寝，就寝。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">本篇前面描摹了许多说话的神态，这里只简单记下两个安静的时候。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-11" name="ly-10-11"></a>

### 10.11　虽疏食菜羹

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">虽疏食菜羹，瓜祭，必齐如也。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">即便只用粗饭、菜汤或瓜果行食前的祭礼，孔子的态度也一定恭敬严肃。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">瓜祭，关于“瓜”字有异说。齐，庄敬严肃的样子。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">食物再粗简，行礼时专注的态度仍可由自己决定。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-12" name="ly-10-12"></a>

### 10.12　席不正

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">席不正，不坐。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">坐席铺摆不端正，孔子就不坐下。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">席，铺在地上供坐卧的席子。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">对秩序的留心，发生在入座之前这个小动作上。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-13" name="ly-10-13"></a>

### 10.13　乡人饮酒

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">乡人饮酒，杖者出，斯出矣。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">在乡里饮酒聚会，等年长持杖的人先出去了，孔子才离开。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">杖者，持杖的年长者。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">宴罢谁先走，也是乡里敬老的一处细节。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-14" name="ly-10-14"></a>

### 10.14　乡人傩

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">乡人傩，朝服而立于阼阶。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">乡人举行驱疫的傩礼，孔子穿着朝服站在东边的台阶上。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">傩（nuó），古代驱除疫鬼的仪式。阼（zuò）阶，东阶。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">经文同时交代了衣着和站位，让人在仪式现场找到孔子的位置。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-15" name="ly-10-15"></a>

### 10.15　问人于他邦

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">问人于他邦，再拜而送之。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">托人向在别国的朋友致问候，孔子会向受托出行的人拜两次，送他上路。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">问人，向人致意问候。再拜，拜两次。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">问候送给远方的人，行礼的对象却是眼前代传的人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-16" name="ly-10-16"></a>

### 10.16　康子馈药

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">康子馈药，拜而受之。曰：“丘未达，不敢尝。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">季康子送来药，孔子拜谢收下，随后说明自己不了解药性，不敢尝用。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">馈，赠送。达，通晓、明白。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">他当面说清迟疑的理由，礼貌收礼仍容得下对药物的审慎。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-17" name="ly-10-17"></a>

### 10.17　厩焚

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">厩焚，子退朝，曰：“伤人乎？”不问马。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">马厩失火，孔子退朝回来，首先问有没有人受伤，没有问马。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">厩（jiù），养马的房舍。退朝，结束朝会而归。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">经文没有记损失多少，只记下孔子得知火情后的第一问。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-18" name="ly-10-18"></a>

### 10.18　君赐食

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">君赐食，必正席先尝之；君赐腥，必熟而荐之；君赐生，必畜之。侍食于君，君祭，先饭。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">君主赐熟食，他端正席位先尝。赐生肉，就煮熟了供奉祖先。赐活物，就养起来。陪君主吃饭，当君主行食前祭礼时，他依侍食之礼先尝饭。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">腥，生肉。荐，进献祭品。畜，饲养。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同样是君主的赏赐，因为到手的东西不同，接下来的动作也不一样。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-19" name="ly-10-19"></a>

### 10.19　疾

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">疾，君视之，东首，加朝服，拖绅。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子患病，君主来探望，他便头朝东躺，在身上覆盖朝服，搭上大带。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">东首，头朝东方。绅，衣冠上的大带。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">病中无法起身穿戴，就用病榻上还能做到的动作表示敬意。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-20" name="ly-10-20"></a>

### 10.20　君命召

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">君命召，不俟驾行矣。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">君主下命召见，孔子不等车马套好就先出发。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">俟，等候。驾，套车备马。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句“不俟”用出门的早晚写出了赴召的态度。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-21" name="ly-10-21"></a>

### 10.21　入太庙

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">入太庙，每事问。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">进入太庙，遇到事情他都询问。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">太庙，此处指鲁国奉祀周公的宗庙。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">熟悉礼的人进入具体礼仪场合，仍有逐事请教的必要。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-22" name="ly-10-22"></a>

### 10.22　朋友死

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">朋友死，无所归，曰：“于我殡。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">朋友去世，如果没有人家可以承接丧事，孔子便说，到我这里停柩治丧。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">殡，入殓后暂时停放灵柩。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">对无处安置的朋友，一声“于我”给出了可以落脚的地方。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-23" name="ly-10-23"></a>

### 10.23　朋友之馈

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">朋友之馈，虽车马，非祭肉，不拜。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">朋友赠礼，即便送来车马，孔子也不因其贵重便行拜礼。若送来的是祭肉，则以拜礼相受。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">祭肉，祭祀后分赠的肉食。馈，赠礼。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">是否拜受，取决于礼物所承载的敬意，不取决于它值多少钱。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-24" name="ly-10-24"></a>

### 10.24　寝不尸

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">寝不尸，居不容。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">睡卧时不像尸体那样僵直，私居时也不摆出会客的仪容。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">容，依此读法是特意整饰的仪容。网页也保留“居不客”的异文。</font></p>

<p title="异文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:0;"><font color="#af9100">原书异文　(居不容 一作：居不客)</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">居家松弛与公共场合的庄重并列起来，本篇所写的人才有完整的日常起居。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-25" name="ly-10-25"></a>

### 10.25　见齐衰者

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">见齐衰者，虽狎，必变。见冕者与瞽者，虽亵，必以貌。凶服者式之，式负版者。有盛馔，必变色而作。迅雷风烈，必变。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">遇服丧的人，即便熟识也改变平日神色。见戴礼冠的人或盲人，虽是常见的熟人，也给以郑重礼貌。乘车遇丧服者或背负国家图籍者，俯身抚车横木致敬。遇丰盛款待，整肃神色起身。突来的雷声烈风也使他神情改变。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">式，同“轼”，车前横木。作动词指凭轼俯身致敬。版，图籍等文书。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“变”字反复出现，写的是身在不同场合能觉察眼前有什么值得郑重对待。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-26" name="ly-10-26"></a>

### 10.26　升车

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">升车，必正立，执绥。车中不内顾，不疾言，不亲指。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">上车前必站端正，握着引绳登车。坐在车上不扭头向车内张望，不急声说话，也不用手随便指点。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">绥（suí），登车时用的绳索。内顾，回首向车内看。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从上车到坐稳，记录者的目光始终跟着身体的方向。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-10-27" name="ly-10-27"></a>

### 10.27　色斯举矣

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">色斯举矣，翔而后集。曰：“山梁雌雉，时哉时哉！”子路共之，三嗅而作。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">鸟一察觉神色有变便飞起，盘旋之后才落下来。孔子感叹山梁上的母野鸡真会把握时机。下句说到子路与野鸡的相遇，有一说是子路向它拱手，野鸡几次振翅便飞走了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">集，鸟停落。“共”“三嗅”历来有不同释读，古注也怀疑末句有缺文。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">起飞与停落都要看准时机，一声“时哉”给全篇的举止神态添了山野里的回响。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-10)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v11" name="ly-v11"></a>

## 先进篇第十一

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-11)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_f22f9365c684.aspx)

<a id="ly-11-01" name="ly-11-01"></a>

### 11.1　先进于礼乐

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“先进于礼乐，野人也；后进于礼乐，君子也。如用之，则吾从先进。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，先学礼乐再入仕的人，原是平民。后来那些先有身份再学礼乐的，是贵族子弟。若要选用，我愿选前一种人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">先进、后进有不同解释，这里依学习礼乐与出仕的先后讲。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句“如用之”把对两类人的比较落到选人才上。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-02" name="ly-11-02"></a>

### 11.2　从我于陈、蔡者

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“从我于陈、蔡者，皆不及门也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子回想，当年跟随他在陈、蔡受困的人，如今都不在身边了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">门，师门。这句也有解为未进仕途的。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">说到昔日的患难，孔子想到的是同去的那些人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-03" name="ly-11-03"></a>

### 11.3　德行

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">德行：颜渊，闵子骞，冉伯牛，仲弓。言语：宰我，子贡。政事：冉有，季路。文学：子游，子夏。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">弟子们各有专长。德行突出的是颜渊、闵子骞、冉伯牛、仲弓。长于辞令的是宰我、子贡。善于治理政务的是冉有、季路。熟悉典籍文献的是子游、子夏。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">文学，在这里指对典籍的学习与传授。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">四类名字放在一起，看得出同门并不必学成同一种人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-04" name="ly-11-04"></a>

### 11.4　回也非助我者也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“回也非助我者也，于吾言无所不说。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，颜回对我的启发可真少啊，我讲什么，他都欢喜领会。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">说，通“悦”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">字面似在埋怨学生不反驳，末句又写出了两人的默契。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-05" name="ly-11-05"></a>

### 11.5　孝哉闵子骞

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“孝哉闵子骞！人不间于其父母昆弟之言。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子称赞闵子骞孝顺，连旁人也挑不出他父母兄弟对他的称赞有什么不当。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">间，非议，挑出不合之处。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">家人最熟悉他的举止，旁人看到的又能与家人的评价相合。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-06" name="ly-11-06"></a>

### 11.6　南容三复白圭

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">南容三复白圭，孔子以其兄之子妻之。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">南容反复吟诵《诗经》中白玉有瑕尚可磨、说错的话难以补救的诗句。孔子把兄长的女儿嫁给了他。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">白圭，白玉制的礼器，这里用来代指《诗经·抑》中的几句诗。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">南容总在重读哪句话，孔子从这件小事看出了他的为人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-07" name="ly-11-07"></a>

### 11.7　季康子问

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">季康子问：“弟子孰为好学？”孔子对曰：“有颜回者好学，不幸短命死矣，今也则亡。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">季康子问哪个弟子爱学习，孔子说颜回爱学，可惜短命而死，如今再没有像他那样的人了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">亡，通“无”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">季康子问的是众弟子，孔子只举了一位已故的人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-08" name="ly-11-08"></a>

### 11.8　颜渊死

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">颜渊死，颜路请子之车以为之椁。子曰：“才不才，亦各言其子也。鲤也死，有棺而无椁，吾不徒行以为之椁。以吾从大夫之后，不可徒行也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">颜回死后，父亲颜路请孔子舍车给儿子置办外棺。孔子说，不论孩子才干如何，做父亲的都疼自己的儿子。我儿子孔鲤死时也只有内棺，我没有卖车步行来给他置外棺。我曾随大夫之后，依礼不能徒步出行。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">椁，套在棺外的外棺。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子用亲生儿子的葬事说明，他对颜回和孔鲤守的是同一尺度。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-09" name="ly-11-09"></a>

### 11.9　颜渊死

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">颜渊死。子曰：“噫！天丧予！天丧予！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">颜回死了，孔子连声哀叹，老天要我的命啊！</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">丧予，使我遭受毁灭般的丧失。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句话接连说了两遍，悲痛就在这重复里。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-10" name="ly-11-10"></a>

### 11.10　颜渊死

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">颜渊死，子哭之恸，从者曰：“子恸矣！”曰：“有恸乎？非夫人之为恸而谁为？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">为颜回痛哭时，随从提醒孔子悲伤过度。他反问，真的过度了吗？不为这个人如此伤痛，我还能为谁？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">恸，极其悲伤，痛哭。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">随从在衡量哭的程度，孔子想到的却是死去的那个人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-11" name="ly-11-11"></a>

### 11.11　颜渊死

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">颜渊死，门人欲厚葬之，子曰：“不可。”门人厚葬之，子曰：“回也视予犹父也，予不得视犹子也。非我也，夫二三子也！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">颜回死后，弟子们坚持厚葬，孔子劝不住。他叹息，颜回待我如父，如今我却不能像为亲子办丧一样待他，这是你们几个的主张啊！</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">二三子，对几位弟子的称呼。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">联系前面孔鲤没有外棺一事，才能读懂“视犹子”的分量。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-12" name="ly-11-12"></a>

### 11.12　季路问事鬼神

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">季路问事鬼神，子曰：“未能事人，焉能事鬼？”曰：“敢问死。”曰：“未知生，焉知死？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路问怎样侍奉鬼神。孔子说，活着的人尚未侍奉好，怎么能侍奉鬼神？子路又请问死，孔子说，生还没弄明白，如何懂得死？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">季路，即仲由，字子路。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两次回答都给子路留下一件眼前可以着手的事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-13" name="ly-11-13"></a>

### 11.13　闵子侍侧

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">闵子侍侧，訚訚如也；子路，行行如也；冉有、子贡，侃侃如也。子乐。“若由也，不得其死然。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">闵子骞在老师身边温和而正直，子路刚强，冉有、子贡和悦从容，孔子见了很高兴。说到子路，他又担心，这样的性情恐怕难得善终。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">訚訚，和悦而能直言的样子。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">众弟子的神情各异，子路的“行行”既传神，也引出了老师的担忧。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-14" name="ly-11-14"></a>

### 11.14　鲁人为长府

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">鲁人为长府，闵子骞曰：“仍旧贯如之何？何必改作？”子曰：“夫人不言，言必有中。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">鲁国人要改建长府，闵子骞问，沿用旧制如何，何必改建呢？孔子说，这人平日不说，一开口就说到点上。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">长府，鲁国的府库。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句“何必”把注意力从怎样改建移到究竟有没有必要。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-15" name="ly-11-15"></a>

### 11.15　由之瑟奚为于丘之门

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“由之瑟奚为于丘之门？”门人不敬子路，子曰：“由也升堂矣，未入于室也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子批评子路的瑟声怎么会出在自己门下。同学因此轻视子路，孔子又说，子路已登堂，只差尚未入室。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">升堂、入室，用由外至内的房屋比喻造诣的层次。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一次批评传到同学那里，竟变成对整个人的轻视，孔子立即纠正。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-16" name="ly-11-16"></a>

### 11.16　子贡问

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡问：“师与商也孰贤？”子曰：“师也过，商也不及。”曰：“然则师愈与？”子曰：“过犹不及。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡问子张与子夏谁更贤，孔子说，一个做过了头，一个做得不够。子贡以为这样子张更好，孔子答，过头和不及一样都偏离了恰当处。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">师是颛孙师，即子张。商是卜商，即子夏。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">只要目标在恰当之处，往前多走未必更接近。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-17" name="ly-11-17"></a>

### 11.17　季氏富于周公

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">季氏富于周公，而求也为之聚敛而附益之。子曰：“非吾徒也，小子鸣鼓而攻之可也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">季氏已经比周公富有，冉求还替他搜取钱财，使他更富。孔子说，他这样已不算我的学生，大家可以公开指责他。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">聚敛，向百姓搜取财物。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">能替主人增加收入的本事，还须问这份收益是从谁身上取得的。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-18" name="ly-11-18"></a>

### 11.18　柴也愚

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">柴也愚，参也鲁，师也辟，由也喭。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">高柴有些愚直，曾参显得迟钝，子张容易偏激，子路显得粗直。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">鲁，迟钝。辟，有解为偏僻、走极端。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">四个字分别点出四个人用功时容易受阻之处。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-19" name="ly-11-19"></a>

### 11.19　回也其庶乎

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“回也其庶乎，屡空。赐不受命而货殖焉，亿则屡中。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，颜回大概已接近道了，却常常穷得一无所有。子贡不甘受命而从事经商，估量行情常常猜中。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">屡空，常陷贫困。亿，揣度。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同是门下高弟，一个常空，一个屡中，对照中保留了两人的不同禀赋。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-20" name="ly-11-20"></a>

### 11.20　子张问善人之道

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张问善人之道，子曰：“不践迹，亦不入于室。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张问善人怎样行事，孔子说，善人即使没有循着前人的脚印，也能向善，却还达不到圣人精深的境界。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">践迹，踏着前人的足迹走。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">上一层不待人教，再上一层却还需要学习，“入室”点出了距离。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-21" name="ly-11-21"></a>

### 11.21　论笃是与

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“论笃是与，君子者乎，色庄者乎？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子问，只凭一个人说话笃实就赞许他，怎知他真是君子，还是仅有庄重的外表？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">与，此处指赞许。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">言谈有分量，是观察的开头，还不能替行为作证。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-11-22" name="ly-11-22"></a>

### 11.22　子路问

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路问：“闻斯行诸？”子曰：“有父兄在，如之何其闻斯行之？”冉有问：“闻斯行诸？”子曰：“闻斯行之。”公西华曰：“由也问闻斯行诸，子曰‘有父兄在’；求也问闻斯行诸，子曰‘闻斯行之’。赤也惑，敢问。”子曰：“求也退，故进之；由也兼人，故退之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路和冉有都问，听到合宜的事马上去做吗？孔子提醒子路要先请示父兄，却叫冉有立即去做。公西华不解。孔子说，冉有好退，所以推他一把。子路勇气过人，所以让他收一收。</font></p>
