<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同样是与亲近的人交往，说话的分寸还要顾及关系的不同。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-13-29" name="ly-13-29"></a>

### 13.29　善人教民七年

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“善人教民七年，亦可以即戎矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，善人教导百姓达到七年，才可以让他们承担军旅之事。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">即戎，从事军旅。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一个“七年”把承担责任前所需的长期准备写了出来。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-13-30" name="ly-13-30"></a>

### 13.30　以不教民战

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“以不教民战，是谓弃之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，让没有受过教导的百姓上阵，这就叫作遗弃他们。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">弃，抛弃，在这里包含不顾其生死之意。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">受到责问的首先是把百姓送上阵的人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-13)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v14" name="ly-v14"></a>

## 宪问篇第十四

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-14)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_9e5afc1a7486.aspx)

<a id="ly-14-01" name="ly-14-01"></a>

### 14.1　宪问耻

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">宪问耻，子曰：“邦有道，谷；邦无道，谷，耻也。”“克、伐、怨、欲不行焉，可以为仁矣？”子曰：“可以为难矣，仁则吾不知也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">原宪问什么是耻。孔子说，国家有道就领俸禄，无道也照领不误，这才可耻。原宪又问，若能使好胜、自夸、怨恨、贪欲都不表现在行动上，算仁吗？孔子说，这已很难得，至于是否就是仁，我还不能说。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">谷，以谷物为俸禄，这里指食禄。伐，自夸。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">克制几种私心已经很难，孔子仍让原宪继续追问仁还有什么内容。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-02" name="ly-14-02"></a>

### 14.2　士而怀居

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“士而怀居，不足以为士矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，士若一味眷恋安逸的住处和生活，就不足以称为士。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">怀居，留恋安适的生活。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“怀”写心里放不下什么，未必取决于眼下住得好不好。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-03" name="ly-14-03"></a>

### 14.3　邦有道

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“邦有道，危言危行；邦无道，危行言孙。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，国家有道，话可以直说，事要正直地做。国家无道，事仍须正直地做，言语却要谦逊审慎。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">危，正直、高峻。孙，通“逊”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两种处境中的“行”没有改动，调整的是开口的分寸。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-04" name="ly-14-04"></a>

### 14.4　有德者必有言

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“有德者必有言，有言者不必有德。仁者必有勇，勇者不必有仁。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有德行的人自然会说出值得听的话，会说的人未必有德。仁者一定有勇气，有勇气的人未必仁爱。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">有言，这里指能说出有分量的话。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两句话都不能倒过来推，能言与勇敢还不足以单独证明一个人的内在品质。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-05" name="ly-14-05"></a>

### 14.5　南宫适问于孔子曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">南宫适问于孔子曰：“羿善射，奡荡舟，俱不得其死然；禹、稷躬稼而有天下。”夫子不答。南宫适出，子曰：“君子哉若人！尚德哉若人！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">南宫适说，羿善射，奡力大能动舟，却都没得善终。禹、稷亲力耕作，天下终归于他们一系。孔子当面没答，等他走了才称赞这人是君子，看重德行。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">尚德，看重德行。稷，相传为周人的先祖。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这位学生挑出哪些故事并排比较，已经透露了他所尊崇的东西。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-06" name="ly-14-06"></a>

### 14.6　君子而不仁者有矣夫

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子而不仁者有矣夫，未有小人而仁者也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子也可能在某件事上没有做到仁，小人却还称不上仁者。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">有矣夫，表示承认这种情形确实存在。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“君子”的称谓并不能替每一个具体举动担保。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-07" name="ly-14-07"></a>

### 14.7　爱之

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“爱之，能勿劳乎？忠焉，能勿诲乎？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">爱一个人，能让他完全不受锻炼吗？真心为他着想，能不劝导他吗？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">劳，使之勤劳、经受磨炼。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">亲爱有时表现为提出要求，尽心有时要靠一句当面的提醒。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-08" name="ly-14-08"></a>

### 14.8　为命

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“为命，裨谌草创之，世叔讨论之，行人子羽修饰之，东里子产润色之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">郑国拟定外交辞令，裨谌起草，世叔研究斟酌，行人子羽修订文辞，住在东里的子产最后润色。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">为命，制作政令，这里指外交辞命。行人，掌外交的官员。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一份对外文书经过四个人的手，每一双手做的事并不一样。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-09" name="ly-14-09"></a>

### 14.9　或问子产

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">或问子产，子曰：“惠人也。”问子西，曰：“彼哉，彼哉！”问管仲，曰：“人也。夺伯氏骈邑三百，饭疏食，没齿无怨言。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有人问子产如何，孔子说，他能给人实惠。问子西，孔子只说“那个人啊”。问管仲，孔子称其了得：他夺去伯氏在骈邑的三百户封地，伯氏此后吃粗饭，直到老死也没有怨言。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">没齿，一生。疏食，粗粮。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">第三个评价用了一个失去封地者的反应，把管仲服人的效果写了出来。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-10" name="ly-14-10"></a>

### 14.10　贫而无怨难

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“贫而无怨难，富而无骄易。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，贫穷而没有怨气很难，富有而不骄傲相对容易。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">易，与前面的“难”相比较而言。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两种处境给人的压力不等，不能只用表面同样平静来衡量。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-11" name="ly-14-11"></a>

### 14.11　孟公绰为赵、魏老则优

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“孟公绰为赵、魏老则优，不可以为滕、薛大夫。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，孟公绰若做晋国赵、魏两家主持家务的长官，绰绰有余。若做滕、薛小国的大夫，却未必适任。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">老，这里是卿大夫的高级家臣。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">家族虽大，国家虽小，职位要求的本领有别，不能只比规模。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-12" name="ly-14-12"></a>

### 14.12　子路问成人

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路问成人，子曰：“若臧武仲之知、公绰之不欲、卞庄子之勇、冉求之艺，文之以礼乐，亦可以为成人矣。”曰：“今之成人者何必然？见利思义，见危授命，久要不忘平生之言，亦可以为成人矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路问什么是成全的人。孔子先举臧武仲的智慧、孟公绰的少欲、卞庄子的勇敢、冉求的才艺，再用礼乐修饰这些长处。随后又说，今天也可从实际选择看：见利先想是否合义，遇到危险肯付出性命，长久困顿或旧约相隔多年仍记得原先的承诺，这也可算成全的人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">成人，在这里指品格才具完备的人。久要，古注有久约、久处困穷等解。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">先拼合四个人的长处，再看一个人遇到利益、危险和旧约时怎么做。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-13" name="ly-14-13"></a>

### 14.13　子问公叔文子于公明贾曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子问公叔文子于公明贾曰：“信乎，夫子不言，不笑，不取乎？”公明贾对曰：“以告者过也。夫子时然后言，人不厌其言；乐然后笑，人不厌其笑；义然后取，人不厌其取。”子曰：“其然？岂其然乎？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子听说公叔文子不说、不笑、不取财物，便向公明贾核实。公明贾说，传闻夸大了。他是在合宜的时候说，真正快乐时笑，合义时才取，所以旁人不嫌他说、笑、取。孔子叹问，真能这样吗？果真如此吗？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">厌，厌烦。信乎，是真的吗。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三个“然后”把令人费解的传闻，改成了可以观察的行为分寸。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-14" name="ly-14-14"></a>

### 14.14　臧武仲以防求为后于鲁

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“臧武仲以防求为后于鲁，虽曰不要君，吾不信也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，臧武仲凭着防邑要求鲁君为他立后人承继家位，虽说这不算要挟君主，我不相信。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">要（yāo），要挟。防，臧氏的封邑。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">话说得是否客气是一回事，说话时手里握着什么筹码是另一回事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-15" name="ly-14-15"></a>

### 14.15　晋文公谲而不正

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“晋文公谲而不正，齐桓公正而不谲。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，晋文公行事多诡诈而不正，齐桓公较为正直，不用那样的诡诈手段。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">谲（jué），诡诈。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两人同为霸主，孔子仍要分辨他们达成目标的方式。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-16" name="ly-14-16"></a>

### 14.16　桓公杀公子纠

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路曰：“桓公杀公子纠，召忽死之，管仲不死，曰未仁乎？”子曰：“桓公九合诸侯不以兵车，管仲之力也。如其仁，如其仁！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路说，齐桓公杀公子纠，召忽为纠殉死，管仲没有，这不是不仁吗？孔子答，桓公多次会合诸侯而不靠兵车，是管仲之力。何等的仁，何等的仁！</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">九合，指多次会合。公子纠，曾与齐桓公争位。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路从管仲未死发问，孔子请他继续追问管仲活下来做成了什么。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-17" name="ly-14-17"></a>

### 14.17　管仲非仁者与

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡曰：“管仲非仁者与？桓公杀公子纠，不能死，又相之。”子曰：“管仲相桓公霸诸侯，一匡天下，民到于今受其赐。微管仲，吾其被发左衽矣。岂若匹夫匹妇之为谅也，自经于沟渎而莫之知也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡也质疑管仲不殉公子纠，还做了齐桓公的国相。孔子说，管仲辅佐桓公联合诸侯、匡正天下，百姓到现在还受益。没有他，我们恐怕已披发左衽。他何必为了狭小的信义，像普通男女那样自尽于沟渠而无人知晓？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">微，如果没有。左衽，衣襟向左掩，反映古人的服制和文化区分。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这次回答把时间推到“至今”，让子贡把后来受益的百姓也算进评价。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-18" name="ly-14-18"></a>

### 14.18　公叔文子之臣大夫僎与文子同升诸公

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">公叔文子之臣大夫僎与文子同升诸公，子闻之,曰：“可以为‘文’矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">公叔文子的家臣僎，后来同他一起在国君朝中任职。孔子听说后认为，公叔文子配得上“文”这个谥号。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">升诸公，到国君朝廷任职。诸，合“之于”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">让从前的下属成为自己的同僚，显出了肯推举人才的胸襟。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-19" name="ly-14-19"></a>

### 14.19　子言卫灵公之无道也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子言卫灵公之无道也，康子曰：“夫如是，奚而不丧？”孔子曰：“仲叔圉治宾客，祝鮀治宗庙，王孙贾治军旅，夫如是，奚其丧？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说卫灵公无道。季康子问，那为什么还没有亡国？孔子说，卫国有仲叔圉主管外交、祝鮀掌宗庙、王孙贾治军旅，有这些人各管其事，怎么会立刻亡国呢？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">宾客，这里主要指诸侯来使等外宾。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从评判君主转到解释国家还能运转，孔子连续举了三位办事的人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-20" name="ly-14-20"></a>

### 14.20　其言之不怍

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“其言之不怍，则为之也难。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，说起大话全不脸红的人，真要做到所说的就很难了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">怍（zuò），惭愧。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">说时若完全没有顾虑，履行所需的功夫便容易被低估。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-21" name="ly-14-21"></a>

### 14.21　陈成子弑简公

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">陈成子弑简公，孔子沐浴而朝，告于哀公曰：“陈恒弑其君，请讨之。”公曰：“告夫三子。”,孔子曰：“以吾从大夫之后，不敢不告也，君曰‘告夫三子’者！”之三子告，不可。孔子曰：“以吾从大夫之后，不敢不告也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">陈恒杀了齐简公。孔子沐浴后上朝，向鲁哀公报告，请求讨伐。哀公叫他去告知三家大夫。孔子感叹，自己既曾列大夫，不敢不报告，国君却把话推给三家。他仍去告知，三家不肯答应。他重申，凭自己曾任的职分，不能不说。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">三子，鲁国季孙、孟孙、叔孙三家之主。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一句“不敢不告”前后各一次，标出了请求虽未被采纳、责任仍须尽到的处境。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-22" name="ly-14-22"></a>

### 14.22　子路问事君

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路问事君，子曰：“勿欺也，而犯之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路问怎样侍奉君主。孔子说，不可欺骗他，遇到应劝谏的事要敢于当面触犯他。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">犯，犯颜进谏，当面指出过失。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">诚实在这里有两面，既不能说假话，也不能把该讲的实话藏起来。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-23" name="ly-14-23"></a>

### 14.23　君子上达

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子上达，小人下达。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子向上通达义理，小人却越来越精于低下的私利。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">达，通达。上、下，指出用心所向。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同样越来越熟练，还须看这种熟练把人引到哪里。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-24" name="ly-14-24"></a>

### 14.24　古之学者为己

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“古之学者为己，今之学者为人。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，古人求学为了充实自己，如今的人求学往往为了让别人看见。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">为己，为了修养自身。为人，在此指为了求得别人称誉。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">别人不在场时还愿不愿意学，可以检验两种用心的差别。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-25" name="ly-14-25"></a>

### 14.25　蘧伯玉使人于孔子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">蘧伯玉使人于孔子，孔子与之坐而问焉，曰：“夫子何为？”对曰：“夫子欲寡其过而未能也。”使者出，子曰：“使乎！使乎！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">蘧伯玉派使者来，孔子请他坐下，问主人近来在做什么。使者说，我家主人想少犯过错，却觉得还没做到。使者离开后，孔子两次赞他真会做使者。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">寡其过，减少自己的过失。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句答话既传出主人的志向，又留住主人未敢自满的态度。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-26" name="ly-14-26"></a>

### 14.26　不在其位

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“不在其位，不谋其政。”曾子曰：“君子思不出其位。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，不在那个职位，不筹划属于那个职位的政务。曾子说，君子的思虑不逾越自己的职分。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">位，承担责任的职位。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两句话一说办事，一说思虑，把界限指向了同一个职分。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-27" name="ly-14-27"></a>

### 14.27　君子耻其言而过其行

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子耻其言而过其行。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子以自己说得比做得多为耻。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">过，超过。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">衡量一句话的尺度，是说话人已有或愿付出的行动。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-28" name="ly-14-28"></a>

### 14.28　君子道者三

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子道者三，我无能焉：仁者不忧，知者不惑，勇者不惧。”子贡曰：“夫子自道也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子之道有三样，我尚未能做到：仁者不忧，智者不惑，勇者不惧。子贡说，老师这正是在说自己。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">自道，自己说出自身的情形。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一段话在老师口中是自勉，在弟子耳中又是对老师的写照。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-29" name="ly-14-29"></a>

### 14.29　子贡方人

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡方人，子曰：“赐也贤乎哉？夫我则不暇。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡拿别人来比较评论，孔子问，赐真有这么富余的本领吗？我自己可没这份闲工夫。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">方人，比较、评量别人。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子没有参加品评，反问起品评者的时间花在了哪里。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-30" name="ly-14-30"></a>

### 14.30　不患人之不己知

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“不患人之不己知，患其不能也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，不担忧别人不了解自己，担忧的是自己没有本领。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">不能，能力不足。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">后一种担忧至少能引出自己可以着手的一件事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-31" name="ly-14-31"></a>

### 14.31　不逆诈

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“不逆诈，不亿不信，抑亦先觉者，是贤乎！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，不先认定别人要骗人，也不凭空猜人不守信，遇上欺骗却能及早觉察，这样的人才贤明。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">逆，预先料定。亿，揣测。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">先入为主的怀疑和明察秋毫，未必是一种本事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-32" name="ly-14-32"></a>

### 14.32　微生亩谓孔子曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">微生亩谓孔子曰：“丘何为是栖栖者与？无乃为佞乎？”孔子曰：“非敢为佞也，疾固也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">微生亩问孔子为何到处奔忙，莫非想卖弄口才？孔子说，我不敢靠花言巧语讨好人，只是厌恶固执不通。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">栖栖，忙碌不得安定的样子。固，固陋。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">对同一种奔走，旁观者看成逞口才，孔子却说起自己想改变的闭塞。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-33" name="ly-14-33"></a>

### 14.33　骥不称其力

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“骥不称其力，称其德也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，千里马被人称道，着眼的是它优良的品性，不能只数它的力气。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">骥，良马。德，此处指优良的性情品质。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">评马尚且要看力气之外的东西，读者也可以照此回看对人的评价。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-34" name="ly-14-34"></a>

### 14.34　或曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">或曰：“以德报怨，何如？”子曰：“何以报德？以直报怨，以德报德。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有人问，以恩惠回报怨仇如何？孔子反问，那又拿什么回报恩惠？应以公正坦直对待怨仇，以恩惠回报恩惠。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">直，公正、合乎情理。德，在此指恩德。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子的反问让原先只谈怨仇的人，把真有恩于己的人也考虑进来。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-35" name="ly-14-35"></a>

### 14.35　莫我知也夫

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“莫我知也夫！”子贡曰：“何为其莫知子也？”子曰：“不怨天，不尤人，下学而上达。知我者其天乎！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子感叹，大概没人理解我吧。子贡问缘故，他说，我不怨天，不责人，从日常人事向上领会道理，知道我的大概只有天了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">尤，归咎、责怪。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“下学”说的是看得见的日常学习，“上达”则是别人未必看得见的追求。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-36" name="ly-14-36"></a>

### 14.36　公伯寮愬子路于季孙

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">公伯寮愬子路于季孙。子服景伯以告，曰：“夫子固有惑志于公伯寮，吾力犹能肆诸市朝。”子曰：“道之将行也与，命也；道之将废也与，命也。公伯寮其如命何？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">公伯寮到季孙那里中伤子路。子服景伯告知孔子，说季孙已受迷惑，自己有力量惩处公伯寮。孔子说，道能推行或被废弃，自有命数，公伯寮能对命怎么样？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">愬，诉说，这里指进谗。肆诸市朝，指陈尸于市。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">景伯把注意力放在处置进谗者上，孔子把他个人能左右的范围缩小了。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-37" name="ly-14-37"></a>

### 14.37　贤者辟世

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“贤者辟世，其次辟地，其次辟色，其次辟言。”子曰：“作者七人矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，贤者有的避开整个乱世，有的离开一地，有的见脸色不对就走，有的听到不当的话才走。已经有七个人这样做了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">辟，通“避”。作，起而离开。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">退出的范围与缘由各有不同，文中并没有给这七人开列姓名。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-38" name="ly-14-38"></a>

### 14.38　子路宿于石门

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路宿于石门，晨门曰：“奚自？”子路曰：“自孔氏。”曰：“是知其不可而为之者与？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路在石门过夜，清晨守门的人问从哪里来。子路说，从孔家来。对方便问，就是那位明知难以办到也仍去做的人吗？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">晨门，清早掌开城门的人。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这句流传很广的评语，最初出自一个没有留下姓名的守门人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-39" name="ly-14-39"></a>

### 14.39　子击磬于卫

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子击磬于卫，有荷蒉而过孔氏之门者，曰：“有心哉，击磬乎！”既而曰：“鄙哉，硁硁乎！莫己知也，斯己而已矣。深则厉，浅则揭。”子曰：“果哉！末之难矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子在卫国敲磬。一个挑筐路过的人先听出他心中有事，继而批评这磬声过于执拗：没人理解就罢了，该像涉水一般，水深穿衣而渡，水浅便提起衣裳。孔子说，这人倒真果断，这样做便没什么难处了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">蒉，草筐。揭，提起衣裳。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">对方听懂了磬声里的坚持，至于要不要放手，两人的选择仍然不同。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-40" name="ly-14-40"></a>

### 14.40　书》云

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张曰：“《书》云，‘高宗谅阴，三年不言。’何谓也？”子曰：“何必高宗，古之人皆然。君薨，百官总己以听于冢宰三年。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张问，《尚书》所说商高宗守丧三年不发政令，是什么意思？孔子答，何止高宗，古人都如此。国君去世，百官各守其职，三年之内听命于冢宰。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">谅阴，古书中指天子居丧。冢宰，统率百官的长官。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">所谓“不言”并非国事无人料理，后半章正说明谁在这段时间主持政务。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-41" name="ly-14-41"></a>

### 14.41　上好礼

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“上好礼，则民易使也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，上位者重视礼，百姓就愿意听从调遣。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">好礼，喜好并践行礼。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">下令前自己怎样待人，也影响百姓接到命令时的态度。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-42" name="ly-14-42"></a>

### 14.42　子路问君子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路问君子，子曰：“修己以敬。”曰：“如斯而已乎？”曰：“修己以安人。”曰：“如斯而已乎？”曰：“修己以安百姓。修己以安百姓，尧、舜其犹病诸！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路问怎样做君子。孔子先说以恭敬修养自己，又说修养自己以使周围的人安定。子路两次追问仅此而已吗，孔子才说，修己以使百姓安定，连尧舜恐怕也觉得难呢。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">病，感到为难。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三次作答都从“修己”起步，变化的是这份工夫应当照顾到多少人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-43" name="ly-14-43"></a>

### 14.43　原壤夷俟

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">原壤夷俟，子曰：“幼而不孙弟，长而无述焉，老而不死，是为贼！”以杖叩其胫。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">原壤叉腿坐着等孔子。孔子责备他，小时不知谦逊敬长，长大没有值得传述的事情，老来还这样，简直贻害于人。说完用手杖叩他的胫骨。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">夷俟，伸腿或蹲踞着等候，在当时属失礼坐姿。胫，小腿。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句极重的责备由一个坐姿引出，针对的是原壤从小到老的行状。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-14-44" name="ly-14-44"></a>

### 14.44　阙党童子将命

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">阙党童子将命，或问之曰：“益者与？”子曰：“吾见其居于位也，见其与先生并行也。非求益者也，欲速成者也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">阙党的一个童子替人传话，有人问这是求上进的孩子吗？孔子说，我见过他坐在成人的位置上，又与长者并排行走。他想尽快得到成人的待遇，还谈不上踏实求进。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">将命，传达言语、指令。先生，这里指长辈。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">急于占据成年人的座位，和日渐具备成年人的修养，原是两件事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-14)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v15" name="ly-v15"></a>

## 卫灵公篇第十五

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-15)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_bdcd3d8cec7c.aspx)

<a id="ly-15-01" name="ly-15-01"></a>

### 15.1　卫灵公问陈于孔子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">卫灵公问陈于孔子，孔子对曰：“俎豆之事，则尝闻之矣；军旅之事，未之学也。”明日遂行。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">卫灵公向孔子请教军阵。孔子答，祭礼中陈列礼器的事，自己倒听说过，行军用兵并没有学过。次日便离开卫国。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">陈，通“阵”。俎豆，古代祭祀用的礼器。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子用一句应答和次日的离去，表明自己愿以什么侍奉国君。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-02" name="ly-15-02"></a>

### 15.2　在陈绝粮

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">在陈绝粮，从者病莫能兴。子路愠见曰：“君子亦有穷乎？”子曰：“君子固穷，小人穷斯滥矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子一行在陈国断粮，随从饿病得起不来。子路带着怨气问，君子也会陷入困境吗？孔子说，君子穷困时仍能守住自己，小人一到穷困便会胡作非为。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">兴，起身。滥，放纵越界。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">君子与小人可能遇见同样的断粮，差别要等到受困以后才看得出。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-03" name="ly-15-03"></a>

### 15.3　赐也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“赐也，女以予为多学而识之者与？”对曰：“然，非与？”曰：“非也，予一以贯之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子问子贡，你以为我只靠广学强记吗？子贡承认，并反问难道不是吗。孔子答，我有一个贯穿这些学问的根本。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">识，读 zhì，记住。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡看到的是老师知道得多，老师请他留意众多知识怎样相通。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-04" name="ly-15-04"></a>

### 15.4　由

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“由，知德者鲜矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子对子路说，真正懂得德的人很少啊。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">鲜，少。由，子路的名。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“知德”二字把难处放在能否认清德行上。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-05" name="ly-15-05"></a>

### 15.5　无为而治者其舜也与

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“无为而治者其舜也与！夫何为哉？恭己正南面而已矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，能不靠繁琐的作为而治好天下的，大概是舜吧！他做了什么？以恭敬的态度端正自身，面向南方在君位上罢了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">南面，古代君主临朝的方位，代指居君位。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">看似静坐，前面仍有“恭己”的工夫。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-06" name="ly-15-06"></a>

### 15.6　子张问行

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张问行，子曰：“言忠信，行笃敬，虽蛮貊之邦行矣；言不忠信，行不笃敬，虽州里行乎哉？立则见其参于前也；在舆则见其倚于衡也，夫然后行。”子张书诸绅。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张问怎样才能处处行得通。孔子说，说话忠实守信，行为诚恳敬慎，到远方异族之地也行得通。做不到，连本乡都未必行得通。站着仿佛看见忠信笃敬在面前，乘车也像看见它靠在车衡上，这才算随时放在心上。子张把话写在大带上。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">衡，车前的横木。绅，束在腰间的大带。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从想象车前的警语到把话写在腰带上，子张给自己安排了一个日常的提醒。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-07" name="ly-15-07"></a>

### 15.7　直哉史鱼

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“直哉史鱼！邦有道如矢，邦无道如矢。君子哉蘧伯玉！邦有道则仕，邦无道则可卷而怀之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子赞史鱼正直，无论国家有没有道，他都像箭一般直。又赞蘧伯玉有君子风度，国家有道便出仕，无道便能将才德收敛起来。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">卷而怀之，像把物品卷起藏在怀里，比喻退藏。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">面对同一政治环境，始终直行与知所进退在这里都受到了称赞。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-08" name="ly-15-08"></a>

### 15.8　可与言而不与之言

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“可与言而不与之言，失人；不可与言而与之言，失言。知者不失人亦不失言。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，有可谈的人却没同他谈，便错过了人。不宜谈却偏要对他说，便白费了话。明智的人既不错失人，也不轻掷言语。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">失人，错过值得谈话的人。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">开不开口，得同时考虑所说的事和面前听话的人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-09" name="ly-15-09"></a>

### 15.9　志士仁人无求生以害仁

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“志士仁人无求生以害仁，有杀身以成仁。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，有志向而守仁德的人，不以伤害仁义的办法换取活命，在生与仁无法兼顾之时，也愿承担守仁的代价。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">成仁，成全自己的仁德。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">本章把选择放在极端境况中，追问一个人最不肯背弃的是什么。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-10" name="ly-15-10"></a>

### 15.10　子贡问为仁

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡问为仁，子曰：“工欲善其事，必先利其器。居是邦也，事其大夫之贤者，友其士之仁者。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡问怎样行仁。孔子以工匠做好活计前先磨利工具作比，告诉他住在一个国家，就要侍从当地贤能的大夫，与有仁德的士人交朋友。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">事，侍奉、跟从。利其器，使工具锋利。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">譬喻落到后半句，才显出日常跟谁共事、结交也是行仁的准备。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-11" name="ly-15-11"></a>

### 15.11　颜渊问为邦

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">颜渊问为邦，子曰：“行夏之时，乘殷之辂，服周之冕，乐则《韶》《舞》；放郑声，远佞人。郑声淫，佞人殆。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">颜渊问治国，孔子主张采用夏代历法，乘殷代的礼车，戴周代的礼帽，音乐则取《韶》舞。同时舍弃郑地过度放纵的乐调，疏远巧言取悦之人，因为一种会过度，一种会招来危险。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">辂，古代大车。冕，礼冠。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">列出的制度分属几个时代，选择时看重的是各有何长。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-12" name="ly-15-12"></a>

### 15.12　人无远虑

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“人无远虑，必有近忧。”</font></p>
