<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，若不为日后着想，眼前很可能就会出现令人担忧的事。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">远虑，为较长远的后果预作考虑。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">现在觉得很近的忧患，往往曾有一段可以提前照看的时候。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-13" name="ly-15-13"></a>

### 15.13　已矣乎

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“已矣乎！吾未见好德如好色者也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子叹息，算了吧，我还没见过爱德行能像爱美色那样迫切的人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">好，读 hào，喜爱。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">他拿人人熟悉的吸引力作尺度，问口中的好德究竟有几分真切。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-14" name="ly-15-14"></a>

### 15.14　臧文仲其窃位者与

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“臧文仲其窃位者与！知柳下惠之贤而不与立也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子问，臧文仲算是白占了职位吧？他明知柳下惠贤能，却不肯让他同在朝廷任事。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">窃位，占据职位却未尽职责。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">在位者的责任也包括让比自己合适的人有机会任事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-15" name="ly-15-15"></a>

### 15.15　躬自厚而薄责于人

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“躬自厚而薄责于人，则远怨矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，严格要求自己，对别人的责备轻一些，就能远离怨恨。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">躬自厚，责求自身较严。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“厚”“薄”各有对象，颠倒了方向，人与人的关系便大不相同。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-16" name="ly-15-16"></a>

### 15.16　不曰‘如之何、如之何’者

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“不曰‘如之何、如之何’者，吾末如之何也已矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，对从不问自己“怎么办、怎么办”的人，我也实在拿他没办法了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">末，无，不能。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">老师愿意帮忙，仍得学生先察觉有一个问题需要自己面对。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-17" name="ly-15-17"></a>

### 15.17　群居终日

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“群居终日，言不及义，好行小慧，难矣哉！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，整天聚在一起，谈话碰不到什么合义的事，却总想施展小聪明，这样就很难有所进益。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">小慧，逞巧取利的小聪明。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">相聚的时间越长，也越看得出谈话把人引向什么方向。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-18" name="ly-15-18"></a>

### 15.18　君子义以为质

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子义以为质，礼以行之，孙以出之，信以成之。君子哉！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子把合义当根本，用礼实行，用谦逊的言辞表达，再靠守信做成，这才称得上君子。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">孙，通“逊”。质，本质、根本。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从判断应不应该做，一直讲到怎样说、怎样兑现，一件事才算完成。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-19" name="ly-15-19"></a>

### 15.19　君子病无能焉

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子病无能焉，不病人之不己知也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">君子担心自身没有足够能力，不担心别人还不了解自己。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">病，忧虑、以之为患。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">在意才干还是在意知名，两种忧虑会引出很不一样的用功方向。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-20" name="ly-15-20"></a>

### 15.20　君子疾没世而名不称焉

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子疾没世而名不称焉。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">君子担心一生终了，竟没有值得后人称述的名声。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">没世，终其一生。称，称述。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">把目光放到生命终点，“名”便联系着究竟做过什么值得称道的事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-21" name="ly-15-21"></a>

### 15.21　君子求诸己

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子求诸己，小人求诸人。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子先向自己求，小人一味向别人求。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">诸，合“之于”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">遇事先把要求放在哪里，两种为人很快就显出差别。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-22" name="ly-15-22"></a>

### 15.22　君子矜而不争

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子矜而不争，群而不党。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子自重庄严，却不同人争高下，能与众人相处，也不拉帮结派。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">矜，庄重自持。党，结成偏私的小团体。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">有自己的分量，不妨碍合群；能够合群，也不必处处袒护自己人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-23" name="ly-15-23"></a>

### 15.23　君子不以言举人

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子不以言举人，不以人废言。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子不因一个人说得动听就推举他，也不因对一个人的看法便丢弃他说得对的话。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">举，推举、任用。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">择人要看行为，听话要看道理，两项判断都不能图省事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-24" name="ly-15-24"></a>

### 15.24　子贡问曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡问曰：“有一言而可以终身行之者乎？”子曰：“其恕乎！己所不欲，勿施于人。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡问，有没有一个字可以终身实践？孔子说，大概是“恕”吧，自己不愿接受的，不要加给别人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">恕，从自己的感受推想到对方。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">每逢要别人承担什么，这个字便有一次重新实践的机会。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-25" name="ly-15-25"></a>

### 15.25　吾之于人也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“吾之于人也，谁毁谁誉？如有所誉者，其有所试矣。斯民也，三代之所以直道而行也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，我评人，哪里肯轻易毁谤、赞美？若有称许，必已用事实检验过。夏商周能行正直之道，所面对的也正是这样的百姓。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">试，检验。三代，夏、商、周。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">把一句称许放回可查验的事实中，才对得起所评的那个人。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-26" name="ly-15-26"></a>

### 15.26　吾犹及史之阙文也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“吾犹及史之阙文也，有马者借人乘之，今亡矣夫！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子感叹，自己还见过史官遇到可疑处就把文字留空，也见过有马的人借给别人骑，如今这些做法已少见了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">阙文，留下空缺。有马借人乘一句，旧说也有解释为请会骑的人调习。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这两件往日小事都触及一种态度：遇上自己把握不准的地方，肯留出余地。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-27" name="ly-15-27"></a>

### 15.27　巧言乱德

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“巧言乱德，小不忍，则乱大谋。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，悦耳的巧言会扰乱德行。小处不能忍耐，会扰乱整个大计。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">巧言，刻意说得动听以讨人喜欢的话。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一个“乱”字，把一时的话语和一时的冲动都接到了较远的后果上。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-28" name="ly-15-28"></a>

### 15.28　众恶之

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“众恶之，必察焉；众好之，必察焉。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，众人都讨厌一个人，要亲自考察。众人都喜欢他，也一样要考察。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">察，仔细核实。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">面对整齐一致的好评，查证的必要丝毫没有减少。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-29" name="ly-15-29"></a>

### 15.29　人能弘道

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“人能弘道，非道弘人。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，人能够把道发扬光大，道却不会自动把一个人变得伟大。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">弘，扩充、发扬。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">原则的力量要看人怎样实行，挂在口头的道还没有替谁做成一件事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-30" name="ly-15-30"></a>

### 15.30　过而不改

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“过而不改，是谓过矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，有了过失还不肯改，这才真正成了过错。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">改，着手纠正已经发生的过失。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">事情发生以后，仍有一次选择会改变这件事的意义。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-31" name="ly-15-31"></a>

### 15.31　吾尝终日不食、终夜不寝以思

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“吾尝终日不食、终夜不寝以思，无益，不如学也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，我曾一整天顾不上吃饭，一整夜顾不上睡觉，光在那里思索，并没有收益，倒不如去学习。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">学，包括向人求教、学习已有的知识经验。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子拿自己的尝试来说，闭门苦想所缺的正是新的材料和指点。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-32" name="ly-15-32"></a>

### 15.32　君子谋道不谋食

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子谋道不谋食。耕也馁在其中矣，学也禄在其中矣。君子忧道不忧贫。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">君子谋求正道，心思不专耗在生计上。耕田也可能挨饿，学习也可能得到俸禄。君子忧虑的是道有没有实行，不是自己会不会贫穷。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">馁，饥饿。禄，任职所得俸给。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">谋划一件事的动机和最后得到的结果，未必可以画上等号。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-33" name="ly-15-33"></a>

### 15.33　知及之

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“知及之，仁不能守之，虽得之，必失之；知及之，仁能守之，不庄以莅之，则民不敬；知及之，仁能守之，庄以莅之，动之不以礼，未善也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，聪明才智足以取得地位，仁德不够就守不住。能得能守，临民却不庄重，百姓不会敬重他。能得、能守、能庄重，动员百姓时又不依礼，还是不够好。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">莅，临，指居位治民。动之，使百姓行动。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">每层都假定前一层已做到，再问实际执政时还会缺什么。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-34" name="ly-15-34"></a>

### 15.34　君子不可小知而可大受也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子不可小知而可大受也，小人不可大受而可小知也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，不见得能从一件小事看尽君子，却能把重任交给他。小人虽不宜承当大任，也可能在小事上见出所长。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">大受，承受重大托付。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一次小考察和一项大托付，所需看清的未必是同一种才能。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-35" name="ly-15-35"></a>

### 15.35　民之于仁也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“民之于仁也，甚于水火。水火，吾见蹈而死者矣，未见蹈仁而死者也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，百姓对仁的需要甚至超过水火。有人踏入水火而丧生，我却没见谁因为实行仁本身而受它的伤害。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">蹈，踏入，后一句引申为实行。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">借水火来说明不可缺少，又点出水火与仁的作用有别。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-36" name="ly-15-36"></a>

### 15.36　当仁不让于师

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“当仁不让于师。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，遇到该行仁的事，即使在老师面前，也不必谦让退后。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">当，面对、遇上。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">敬师的谦让遇到当行的义务，不能成为自己袖手的理由。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-37" name="ly-15-37"></a>

### 15.37　君子贞而不谅

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“君子贞而不谅。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，君子守正道，而不拘泥于一字一句的狭小信用。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">贞，守正。谅，这里偏指不辨是非的小信。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">要守住一项承诺，也得记住当初那项承诺是否合义。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-38" name="ly-15-38"></a>

### 15.38　事君

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“事君，敬其事而后其食。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，侍奉君主，先把职事认真做好，把考虑自身俸禄的事放在后面。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">食，指俸禄。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">次序透露一个人把职位首先看成责任还是收入。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-39" name="ly-15-39"></a>

### 15.39　有教无类

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“有教无类。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，在施教时，不因学生原来属于哪一类人而区别对待。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">类，指人群原有的类别、差别。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">短短四字首先要求教者检点，自己曾把哪些人挡在门外。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-40" name="ly-15-40"></a>

### 15.40　道不同

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“道不同，不相为谋。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，所选择的道路不同，便难以共同谋划事情。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">谋，共同商议、筹划。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">要走向哪里尚无共识，很难商量下一步怎样走。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-41" name="ly-15-41"></a>

### 15.41　辞达而已矣

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“辞达而已矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，措辞能把意思传达到就可以了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">达，通达，使对方听明白。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句话够不够好，要看听者是否收到了真正想说的意思。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-15-42" name="ly-15-42"></a>

### 15.42　师冕见

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">师冕见，及阶，子曰：“阶也。”及席，子曰：“席也。”皆坐，子告之曰：“某在斯，某在斯。”师冕出。子张问曰：“与师言之道与？”子曰：“然，固相师之道也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">盲乐师冕来见。走到台阶，孔子提醒有阶。走到席边，又提示席到了。大家坐好，孔子逐个说清谁坐在哪里。乐师走后，子张问这是与乐师说话的方式吗？孔子说，是的，本就是引导、帮助乐师应有的办法。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">相，辅助、引导。师，乐师。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句“某在斯”让看不见席次的人也能参与席间交往，礼体现在这种细处。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-15)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v16" name="ly-v16"></a>

## 季氏篇第十六

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-16)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_2722f42ccaaa.aspx)

<a id="ly-16-01" name="ly-16-01"></a>

### 16.1　季氏将伐颛臾

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">季氏将伐颛臾。冉有、季路见于孔子曰：“季氏将有事于颛臾。”孔子曰：“求！无乃尔是过与？夫颛臾，昔者先王以为东蒙主，且在邦域之中矣，是社稷之臣也。何以伐为？”冉有曰：“夫子欲之，吾二臣者皆不欲也。”孔子曰：“求！周任有言曰：‘陈力就列，不能者止。’危而不持，颠而不扶，则将焉用彼相矣？且尔言过矣，虎兕出于柙，龟玉毁于椟中，是谁之过与？”冉有曰：“今夫颛臾，固而近于费。今不取，后世必为子孙忧。”孔子曰：“求！君子疾夫舍曰欲之而必为之辞。丘也闻有国有家者，不患寡而患不均，不患贫而患不安。盖均无贫，和无寡，安无倾。夫如是，故远人不服，则修文德以来之。既来之，则安之。今由与求也，相夫子，远人不服而不能来也，邦分崩离析而不能守也；而谋动干戈于邦内。吾恐季孙之忧，不在颛臾，而在萧墙之内也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">季氏准备进攻颛臾。冉有和季路来告诉孔子。孔子责问冉有：颛臾受先王委任主持东蒙山祭祀，本就在鲁国境内，凭什么去打它？冉有说，这是季孙的意思，他们二人并不赞同。孔子引周任的话说，有能力就任职，做不到就退下来。人将跌倒却不伸手扶，还要辅佐者做什么？猛兽逃出笼子、宝玉毁在匣中，照看它们的人能没有责任吗？冉有又说，颛臾城坚，离季氏的费邑近，今日不取恐为子孙忧。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">孔子批评他想做一件事却另找说辞。治国治家应重分配的均平、人心的和顺和处境的安定。远人不服，可修文德来招徕，来后要使他们安心。如今二人既不能招来远人，也不能在邦国分裂时守住局面，却要在国内兴兵。季孙真正的忧患，恐怕就在自家的屏墙之内。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">相，在“焉用彼相”中指扶持盲者的人，借喻辅臣。萧墙，宫室门内的屏墙。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">冉有先说并不赞成，后来却详述动兵的好处。孔子正是在这两次答话之间追究他的责任。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-02" name="ly-16-02"></a>

### 16.2　天下有道

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“天下有道，则礼乐征伐自天子出；天下无道，则礼乐征伐自诸侯出。自诸侯出，盖十世希不失矣；自大夫出，五世希不失矣；陪臣执国命，三世希不失矣。天下有道，则政不在大夫。天下有道，则庶人不议。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，天下有道时，制定礼乐和决定征伐的权力在天子。失道时，这些事由诸侯作主。权柄下移到诸侯，少有传十代还不丢的。下移到大夫，少有过五代的。家臣主持国政，少有过三代的。有道之时，政权不会落在大夫手里，普通百姓也不至于非议政事。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">陪臣，诸侯大夫的家臣。希，少。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">十世、五世、三世依次缩短，写出了孔子所见权柄下移后的不稳。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-03" name="ly-16-03"></a>

### 16.3　禄之去公室五世矣

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“禄之去公室五世矣，政逮于大夫四世矣，故夫三桓之子孙微矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，鲁君失去掌握禄位的权力，已有五代。实权落在大夫手上，也已有四代。因此三桓的子孙如今也衰微了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">三桓，鲁国出自桓公的孟孙、叔孙、季孙三家。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">承上章看，曾从公室手中取得权力的人，也可能守不住自己的权力。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-04" name="ly-16-04"></a>

### 16.4　益者三友

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“益者三友，损者三友。友直，友谅，友多闻，益矣。友便辟，友善柔，友便佞，损矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">有三种朋友对人有益：正直的人、诚实守信的人、见闻广博的人。有三种朋友对人有损：专讲逢迎的人、当面柔顺讨好的人、靠巧言取悦的人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">谅，诚信。便佞，善用花言巧语。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两组三友都和交谈有关：能告诉你实情，与只说你爱听的话，会把你引向不同的地方。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-05" name="ly-16-05"></a>

### 16.5　益者三乐

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“益者三乐，损者三乐。乐节礼乐，乐道人之善，乐多贤友，益矣。乐骄乐，乐佚游，乐晏乐，损矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，喜欢以礼乐调节自己，喜欢谈别人的长处，喜欢结交贤友，这三种爱好有益。以骄纵为乐，以闲散游荡为乐，以宴饮无度为乐，这三种爱好有害。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">乐，作“喜爱”时读 yào。晏乐，宴饮享乐。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这一章关心的既是做了什么，也是从什么事上感到快意。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-06" name="ly-16-06"></a>

### 16.6　侍于君子有三愆

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“侍于君子有三愆：言未及之而言谓之躁，言及之而不言谓之隐，未见颜色而言谓之瞽。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">在君子身边交谈容易有三种过失。还没轮到便抢着说，叫急躁。轮到自己却有话不说，叫隐瞒。不看对方神色就径自开口，简直如同看不见。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">愆，过失。颜色，这里指人的神色。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">同一句话说早、说迟，或没看见别人正在为难，都会失掉分寸。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-07" name="ly-16-07"></a>

### 16.7　君子有三戒

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“君子有三戒：少之时，血气未定，戒之在色；及其壮也，血气方刚，戒之在斗；及其老也，血气既衰，戒之在得。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，人在少年时血气未定，应警惕对美色的迷恋。壮年血气方盛，应戒好斗。老年血气渐衰，应戒贪得。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">得，这里指一味求取、占有。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三句话按年龄分开，也各自给出一个需要留心的欲望。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-08" name="ly-16-08"></a>

### 16.8　君子有三畏

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“君子有三畏：畏天命，畏大人，畏圣人之言。小人不知天命而不畏也，狎大人，侮圣人之言。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">君子对天命、居高位或有大德的人以及圣人的话存有敬畏。小人不了解天命，便不敬畏，对大人轻慢，对圣人的话随意侮弄。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">狎，过分亲近以至失敬。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">在这一章里，“知”在“畏”之前：要先理解分量，才谈得上郑重对待。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-09" name="ly-16-09"></a>

### 16.9　生而知之者

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“生而知之者，上也；学而知之者，次也；困而学之，又其次也；困而不学，民斯为下矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子把生来便知道的人列为上等，靠学习而知道的次之，遇上难处才肯学习的又次之。遇到困难依旧拒绝学习，才排在最末。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">困，行事不通、遭遇疑难。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">后两类人遇到了一样的困难，真正使他们分开的，是接下来学还是不学。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-10" name="ly-16-10"></a>

### 16.10　君子有九思

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“君子有九思：视思明，听思聪，色思温，貌思恭，言思忠，事思敬，疑思问，忿思难，见得思义。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">君子有九件事常放在心上：看要看清，听要听明，神色要温和，仪态要恭敬，言语要真诚，做事要郑重，有疑要发问，生气要想到可能招来的麻烦，碰上利益要问合不合义。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">忿思难，气愤时想到后患。“难”在这里读 nàn。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">末两项特别挑出人最容易来不及思量的时刻：动怒和见利。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-11" name="ly-16-11"></a>

### 16.11　见善如不及

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“见善如不及，见不善如探汤。吾见其人矣，吾闻其语矣。隐居以求其志，行义以达其道。吾闻其语矣，未见其人也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，看见善行便生怕赶不上，碰见不善像手探入热水般马上缩回。这样的人我见过，这样的话我也听过。至于退隐时坚持志向，行义时实现所信的道，这话听说过，榜样我还没见到。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">探汤，把手伸进热水。“汤”是热水。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">留意两次“闻”都保留了，第二次的“见”却落了空。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-12" name="ly-16-12"></a>

### 16.12　齐景公有马千驷

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">齐景公有马千驷，死之日，民无德而称焉。伯夷叔齐饿于首阳之下，民到于今称之。其斯之谓与？</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">齐景公有足以编成千组四匹马的财富，死后百姓却说不出什么德行值得称颂。伯夷、叔齐在首阳山下挨饿，百姓至今还称道他们。大概说的就是这种分别吧。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">驷，四匹马为一组。首阳，传说伯夷、叔齐隐居之山。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">开头数得出景公的马，到他身后，要数的却变成了人们记得的德行。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-13" name="ly-16-13"></a>

### 16.13　陈亢问于伯鱼曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">陈亢问于伯鱼曰：“子亦有异闻乎？”对曰：“未也。尝独立，鲤趋而过庭。曰：‘学诗乎？’对曰：‘未也’。‘不学诗，无以言。’鲤退而学诗。他日又独立，鲤趋而过庭。曰：‘学礼乎？’对曰：‘未也’。‘不学礼，无以立。’鲤退而学礼。闻斯二者。”陈亢退而喜曰：“问一得三：闻诗，闻礼，又闻君子之远其子也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">陈亢问孔鲤，父亲是否私下传授过什么特别的东西。孔鲤说没有，只有两次快步经过庭院，父亲分别问他学过《诗》和礼没有，并告诉他不学《诗》便难以言谈，不学礼便难以立身。他于是各自去学。陈亢高兴地说，本想知道一件事，却知道了三件：学诗、学礼，还知道君子对亲生儿子也保持分寸。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">伯鱼，孔子之子孔鲤的字。趋，快步走，以示恭敬。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">陈亢起初想找一份特别的传授，最后记住的第三件事恰是父子间的距离。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-16-14" name="ly-16-14"></a>

### 16.14　邦君之妻

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">邦君之妻，君称之曰“夫人”，夫人自称曰“小童”；邦人称之曰“君夫人”，称诸异邦曰“寡小君”；异邦人称之亦曰“君夫人”。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">邦君称自己的妻子为“夫人”，她自称“小童”。本国人对她称“君夫人”，向外国人说起她称“寡小君”。别国的人称她，也说“君夫人”。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">寡小君，本国人对外谦称国君的妻子。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">称呼随着说话人和听话人而变；把每一句放回谁在对谁说，就不难分清。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-16)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v17" name="ly-v17"></a>

## 阳货篇第十七

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-17)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_27c1627899ca.aspx)

<a id="ly-17-01" name="ly-17-01"></a>

### 17.1　阳货欲见孔子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">阳货欲见孔子，孔子不见，归孔子豚。孔子时其亡也而往拜之，遇诸涂。谓孔子曰：“来，予与尔言。”曰：“怀其宝而迷其邦，可谓仁乎？”曰：“不可。”“好从事而亟失时，可谓知乎？”曰：“不可！”“日月逝矣，岁不我与！”孔子曰：“诺，吾将仕矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">阳货想见孔子，孔子避而不见。阳货送来一只小猪，孔子特意挑他外出时回拜，却在路上碰见了。阳货问，把才干藏起来，任凭国家迷乱，能算仁吗？想有作为，却屡次错失时机，能算明智吗？他说岁月不等人。孔子最后答应说，自己会出来任职。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">归，通“馈”，赠送。豚，小猪。时其亡，选在他不在的时候。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子确实说了“将仕”，谈话却没有说他会到阳货手下任职。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-02" name="ly-17-02"></a>

### 17.2　性相近也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“性相近也，习相远也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，人原有的禀性相近，后来习惯和学习的不同，才使人渐渐相差很远。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">习，后天反复学习、习染而成的习惯。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两句话用了方向相反的“近”和“远”，把变化放在了后天的生活里。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-03" name="ly-17-03"></a>

### 17.3　唯上知与下愚不移

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“唯上知与下愚不移。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，只有智慧最高和愚昧最深的两端不容易改变。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">上知，“知”通“智”，上知指最有智慧的人。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">承接上一章读，“唯”字让人留意孔子在这里特别挑出的是两端。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-04" name="ly-17-04"></a>

### 17.4　子之武城

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子之武城，闻弦歌之声。夫子莞尔而笑，曰：“割鸡焉用牛刀？”子游对曰：“昔者偃也闻诸夫子曰：‘君子学道则爱人，小人学道则易使也。’”子曰：“二三子，偃之言是也！前言戏之耳。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子到武城听到弦乐和歌声，微笑说，杀鸡何必用宰牛的刀？任当地长官的子游答，先生以前教过，君子学道会爱人，普通人学道也容易服从教导。孔子便对学生们说，子游说得对，我刚才是和他开个玩笑。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">偃，子游的名。弦歌，伴着弦乐歌唱，此处关乎礼乐教化。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子游用老师平日的话回答老师，孔子也当着学生的面承认他说得有理。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-05" name="ly-17-05"></a>

### 17.5　公山弗扰以费畔

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">公山弗扰以费畔，召，子欲往。子路不说，曰：“末之也已，何必公山氏之之也？”子曰：“夫召我者而岂徒哉？如有用我者，吾其为东周乎！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">公山弗扰据费邑反叛，派人来请孔子，孔子想去。子路很不高兴，说，实在无处可去就算了，何必要去他那里？孔子答，他叫我，总会有个缘由吧。倘若真有人肯用我，我或许能在东方重兴周道。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">畔，通“叛”。费，鲁国季氏的采邑，旧读 bì。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路问的是投奔谁，孔子的回答落在若能任事，自己想做什么。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-06" name="ly-17-06"></a>

### 17.6　子张问仁于孔子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张问仁于孔子，孔子曰：“能行五者于天下为仁矣。”请问之，曰：“恭、宽、信、敏、惠。恭则不侮，宽则得众，信则人任焉，敏则有功，惠则足以使人。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张问仁。孔子说，能随处实行恭、宽、信、敏、惠五件事，可称为仁。恭敬可免受轻侮，宽厚能得到拥护，守信会受人信赖，勤敏能把事办成，肯给人实惠才有条件用人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">人任焉，人肯信任他。敏，勤快敏捷。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">五个德目后面各跟着一个结果，可以逐项看它怎样改变人与人的关系。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-07" name="ly-17-07"></a>

### 17.7　佛肸召

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">佛肸召，子欲往。子路曰：“昔者由也闻诸夫子曰：‘亲于其身为不善者，君子不入也。’佛肸以中牟畔，子之往也，如之何？”子曰：“然，有是言也。不曰坚乎，磨而不磷；不曰白乎，涅而不缁。吾岂匏瓜也哉？焉能系而不食？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">佛肸据中牟反叛，邀请孔子，孔子想去。子路提醒他，从前您说过，亲自做坏事的人那里，君子不去，如今怎么解释？孔子承认说过，又问，真正坚硬的东西怎会一磨就薄，真正洁白的东西怎会一染就黑？我难道要像一个挂起来没人吃的葫芦，始终派不上用场吗？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">佛肸，读 bì xī。磷，磨薄。缁，染黑。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">前两个譬喻回应会不会变质，末一个葫芦却问一直闲置会怎样。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-08" name="ly-17-08"></a>

### 17.8　由也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“由也，女闻六言六蔽矣乎？”对曰：“未也。”“居！吾语女。好仁不好学，其蔽也愚；好知不好学，其蔽也荡；好信不好学，其蔽也贼；好直不好学，其蔽也绞；好勇不好学，其蔽也乱；好刚不好学，其蔽也狂。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子请仲由坐下，告诉他六种美德各有可能受蒙蔽的地方。爱仁而不肯学，容易愚昧。爱智而不学，容易放荡无归。爱信而不学，可能拘守小信反而害人。爱直而不学，话会尖刻。爱勇而不学，容易闹出乱子。爱刚强而不学，就会流于狂妄。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">蔽，遮蔽，此指长处因缺少学习而产生的偏失。绞，急切苛厉。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">六句话都在长处后加上同一个条件：“不好学”。问题也就在这里发生。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-09" name="ly-17-09"></a>

### 17.9　小子何莫学夫《诗

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“小子何莫学夫《诗》？《诗》可以兴，可以观，可以群，可以怨。迩之事父，远之事君，多识于鸟兽草木之名。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子问弟子们何不学《诗》。《诗》能激发情志、观察风俗、使人相互切磋，也能寄托怨意。近可用于侍奉父亲，远可用于事奉君主，还能认识许多鸟兽草木的名称。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">兴，感发情志。群，与人相处、交流切磋。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">从表达怨意一直说到辨认草木，孔子把读诗的用处分得很细。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-10" name="ly-17-10"></a>

### 17.10　子谓伯鱼曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子谓伯鱼曰：“女为《周南》、《召南》矣乎？人而不为《周南》、《召南》，其犹正墙面而立也与！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子问儿子伯鱼，你学过《周南》《召南》了吗？人若没学过这两组诗，就像面对着墙站立，眼前难以展开，也难向前走。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">《周南》《召南》，《诗经》国风开头的两组诗。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子把没读过诗造成的局促，写成了一个可以亲身想象的姿势。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-11" name="ly-17-11"></a>

### 17.11　礼云礼云

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“礼云礼云，玉帛云乎哉？乐云乐云，钟鼓云乎哉？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子问，人们反复说“礼”，难道指的只是玉器和丝帛吗？反复说“乐”，难道指的只是钟鼓吗？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">玉帛，行礼时所用的玉器和丝织品。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两个反问都停在器物上，留给读者去问：器物齐备以后，还缺什么？</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-12" name="ly-17-12"></a>

### 17.12　色厉而内荏

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“色厉而内荏，譬诸小人，其犹穿窬之盗也与？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子把外表强硬、内里怯弱的人，比作偷偷穿墙翻墙的小贼，表面那股威势并无可靠的内在凭据。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">荏，软弱。窬，越墙或穿过墙洞。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“色”和“内”先分开了人的外表与内心，后面的比喻再照出他的心虚。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-13" name="ly-17-13"></a>

### 17.13　乡愿

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“乡愿，德之贼也。”</font></p>
