<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，一乡人眼中处处讨好、看似厚道的人，反会损害真正的德行。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">乡愿，指迎合乡里、以忠厚名声取悦众人的人。贼，这里指损害者。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">此章值得和《孟子·尽心下》万章问乡原一段对读，看为什么挑不出毛病也可能有问题。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-14" name="ly-17-14"></a>

### 17.14　道听而涂说

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“道听而涂说，德之弃也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，在路上听来一句，转头就在路上传出去，这样会把本可以涵养的德行丢掉。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">涂，通“途”。说，在这里指转述。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“道”和“涂”都是路，从听到说几乎没有间隔。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-15" name="ly-17-15"></a>

### 17.15　鄙夫可与事君也与哉

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“鄙夫可与事君也与哉？其未得之也，患得之；既得之，患失之。苟患失之，无所不至矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子问，鄙陋的人能与他一起侍奉君主吗？没得到职位时，他发愁怎样弄到手。得到了，又忧心会丢。若一心只想着不能失去，还有什么事做不出来？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">患得之，忧虑自己能否得到。事君，事奉君主。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">所患的对象从得变成失，对利禄的依恋却始终没有变。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-16" name="ly-17-16"></a>

### 17.16　古者民有三疾

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“古者民有三疾，今也或是之亡也。古之狂也肆，今之狂也荡；古之矜也廉，今之矜也忿戾；古之愚也直，今之愚也诈而已矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子感叹，古人有三种毛病，如今连毛病的样子也变了。古人的狂是率意不拘，如今的狂成了放荡无度。古人的矜持还有界限，如今却变成暴躁乖戾。古人的愚尚有直率，如今的愚竟夹着欺诈。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">矜，持守自尊。廉，在这里指有棱角、有界限。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">三组今昔对照都问：一种不完满的性情里，还保留着什么可取之处？</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-17" name="ly-17-17"></a>

### 17.17　巧言令色

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“巧言令色，鲜矣仁。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，只凭花巧的言辞和特意讨好的脸色待人，很少与仁相应。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">令色，刻意做出惹人喜欢的神色。鲜，少。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这句话在《学而》也出现过。重读时可以留意“鲜”字下判断的分寸。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-18" name="ly-17-18"></a>

### 17.18　恶紫之夺朱也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“恶紫之夺朱也，恶郑声之乱雅乐也，恶利口之覆邦家者。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子厌恶紫色夺了正色朱红的位置，也厌恶郑地的音乐扰乱雅乐，更厌恶巧舌利口之人使国家倾覆。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">覆邦家，使国家倾覆。朱，正红色。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">色、声、言依次出现，最末一句才见出这种错乱在政治上的代价。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-19" name="ly-17-19"></a>

### 17.19　予欲无言

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“予欲无言。”子贡曰：“子如不言，则小子何述焉？”子曰：“天何言哉？四时行焉，百物生焉，天何言哉？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说想不再讲话。子贡问，您若不说，我们这些学生靠什么传述？孔子问，天说过什么？四季照样运行，万物照样生长。天又说过什么？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">述，传述、转达所学。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡等着可以传下去的话，孔子却要他亲自看四季与万物。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-20" name="ly-17-20"></a>

### 17.20　孺悲欲见孔子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孺悲欲见孔子，孔子辞以疾。将命者出户，取瑟而歌，使之闻之。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孺悲求见孔子，孔子让人以生病推辞。传话人刚走出门，他就拿起瑟唱歌，特意让孺悲听见。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">将命者，替人传达话语的人。瑟，弦乐器。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">特意让门外听见的歌声，比一句托病的答复多传达了一层意思，文中没有说明拒见的缘由。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-21" name="ly-17-21"></a>

### 17.21　宰我问

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">宰我问：“三年之丧，期已久矣！君子三年不为礼，礼必坏；三年不为乐，乐必崩。旧谷既没，新谷既升，钻燧改火，期可已矣。”子曰：“食夫稻，衣夫锦，于女安乎？”曰：“安！”“女安则为之！夫君子之居丧，食旨不甘，闻乐不乐，居处不安，故不为也。今女安，则为之！”宰我出，子曰：“予之不仁也！子生三年，然后免于父母之怀。夫三年之丧，天下之通丧也，予也有三年之爱于其父母乎！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">宰我觉得守丧一年已经够久：三年不行礼乐，礼乐会坏。旧谷吃完、新谷登场，连取火所用的木材也已轮换一周。孔子不先谈年数，只问他，父母去世不久就吃精米、穿锦衣，心安吗？宰我答心安。孔子说，那就照你的意思做。君子在丧中吃好东西不觉甘美，听音乐不觉快乐，住得也不安，所以不这样做。宰我走后，孔子感叹他不仁，孩子生下来约三年才离得开父母的怀抱。天下通行三年之丧，难道他没有得到过父母三年的爱吗？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">期，读 jī，一整年。钻燧改火，按时节换木取火。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">宰我以谷物和火种计算一年，孔子请他回想的却是婴儿在怀里的那段时间。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-22" name="ly-17-22"></a>

### 17.22　饱食终日

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“饱食终日，无所用心，难矣哉！不有博弈者乎？为之犹贤乎已。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，成天吃饱了却什么也不肯用心，这样很难有所长进。不是还有棋戏可下吗？做这些也比全然闲着好。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">博弈，古代的棋戏。已，在这里指什么也不做。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">下棋在这里被拿来作最低限度的比较，切入口是人还肯不肯用心。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-23" name="ly-17-23"></a>

### 17.23　君子尚勇乎

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路曰：“君子尚勇乎？”子曰：“君子义以为上。君子有勇而无义为乱，小人有勇而无义为盗。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路问君子是否崇尚勇敢。孔子说，君子把义放在首位。有地位的人有勇无义会作乱，普通人有勇无义则可能以强力行窃。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">尚，推崇。义，衡量行为是否合宜的准则。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路原先只问勇，回答却先给勇找了一个衡量方向的尺度。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-24" name="ly-17-24"></a>

### 17.24　君子亦有恶乎

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡曰：“君子亦有恶乎？”子曰：“有恶。恶称人之恶者，恶居下流而讪上者，恶勇而无礼者，恶果敢而窒者。”曰：“赐也亦有恶乎？”“恶徼以为知者，恶不孙以为勇者，恶讦以为直者。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡问君子也有厌恶的人吗。孔子说，有：到处宣扬别人恶处的人，身居下位却专毁谤上面的人，勇而无礼的人，以及敢做敢断却不通情理的人。孔子再问子贡。子贡说，我厌恶窃取别人的见解来冒充聪明的人，把不谦逊当勇敢的人，和揭发隐私却自以为正直的人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">徼，此处取窃取义。讦，揭人隐私或过失。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子问回学生，子贡列出的三项都有伪装：外面打着一种美德的名号。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-25" name="ly-17-25"></a>

### 17.25　唯女子与小人为难养也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“唯女子与小人为难养也，近之则不逊，远之则怨。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，女子与小人难以相处：过于亲近就不谦逊，疏远了又会生怨。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">养，此处常释作对待、相处。“小人”所指历来有身份与品行等不同解释。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">这句话反映了古代以身份和性别论人的局限。读时可以追问，谁在决定亲疏，谁又有机会说出自己的感受。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-17-26" name="ly-17-26"></a>

### 17.26　年四十而见恶焉

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子曰：“年四十而见恶焉，其终也已。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，一个人到了四十岁仍为人所厌恶，恐怕此后也难有什么转变了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">见恶，被人厌恶。“见”表示被动。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“年四十”把前面的日积月累也算在了这声叹息里。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-17)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v18" name="ly-v18"></a>

## 微子篇第十八

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-18)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_5a6cd9a5210f.aspx)

<a id="ly-18-01" name="ly-18-01"></a>

### 18.1　微子去之

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">微子去之，箕子为之奴，比干谏而死。孔子曰：“殷有三仁焉。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">殷商末年，微子离开了，箕子沦为奴隶，比干因进谏而死。孔子说，殷有三位仁人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">微子、箕子、比干，都是殷商王族中人。谏，向君主直言劝告。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一人离去，一人忍辱，一人死谏；并列的三件事让“仁”有了三种面貌。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-02" name="ly-18-02"></a>

### 18.2　柳下惠为士师

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">柳下惠为士师，三黜。人曰：“子未可以去乎？”曰：“直道而事人，焉往而不三黜？枉道而事人，何必去父母之邦？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">柳下惠担任掌刑狱的官，三次被罢免。有人问何不离开鲁国。他答，若依正道做事，去哪里能保证不被罢免三次？若肯屈从迎合，又何必离开父母之邦？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">士师，掌管刑狱的官。黜，罢免。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">别人劝他换一个国家，他先问自己肯不肯换一种做事的方法。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-03" name="ly-18-03"></a>

### 18.3　齐景公待孔子曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">齐景公待孔子曰：“若季氏，则吾不能。”以季、孟之间待之，曰：“吾老矣，不能用也。”孔子行。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">齐景公谈到怎样礼遇孔子，说自己做不到像对待季氏那样，打算给他介于鲁国季氏与孟氏之间的待遇。后来又说自己老了，不能任用他。孔子便离开了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">季氏、孟氏，鲁国的卿大夫家族，地位有别。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">景公说了两次怎样“待”孔子，孔子听到不能任用便动身了。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-04" name="ly-18-04"></a>

### 18.4　齐人归女乐

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">齐人归女乐，季桓子受之，三日不朝，孔子行。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">齐国人把一批女乐送给鲁国，季桓子收下，连续三天不理朝政，孔子便离开了鲁国。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">归，通“馈”，馈赠。女乐，指表演歌舞的女子。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">全章只叙赠礼、受礼、废朝、离开四步，没有为孔子的去留另写一句评语。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-05" name="ly-18-05"></a>

### 18.5　楚狂接舆歌而过孔子曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">楚狂接舆歌而过孔子曰：“凤兮凤兮，何德之衰？往者不可谏，来者犹可追。已而已而，今之从政者殆而！”孔子下，欲与之言，趋而辟之，不得与之言。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">楚国的接舆唱着歌从孔子身边经过，以凤凰称呼他，叹德运衰微。往事已无法挽回，来日还可以赶上，快收手吧，如今从政危险。孔子下车想和他说话，接舆却快步避开，没能谈成。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">接舆，楚国隐者。辟，通“避”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">接舆把话唱给孔子听，却不愿停下来听他的回答。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-06" name="ly-18-06"></a>

### 18.6　长沮、桀溺耦而耕

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">长沮、桀溺耦而耕，孔子过之，使子路问津焉。长沮曰：“夫执舆者为谁？”子路曰：“为孔丘。”曰：“是鲁孔丘与？”曰：“是也。”曰：“是知津矣。”问于桀溺，桀溺曰：“子为谁？”曰：“为仲由。”曰：“是鲁孔丘之徒与？”对曰：“然。”曰：“滔滔者天下皆是也，而谁以易之？且而与其从辟人之士也，岂若从辟世之士？”耰而不辍。子路行以告，夫子怃然曰：“鸟兽不可与同群，吾非斯人之徒与而谁与？天下有道，丘不与易也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">长沮、桀溺并肩耕作。孔子让子路去问渡口。长沮一听执缰的是鲁国孔丘，就说，他应该知道渡口。桀溺问明子路的身份，便说天下到处都是这般乱象，你们跟谁去改变它？跟着躲避坏人的人，不如跟着避开整个世道的人。他说着继续耕田。子路回来报告，孔子怅然说，人不能跟鸟兽过日子，我不和世人相处，又和谁相处？如果天下已经有道，我就用不着参与改变了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">问津，询问渡口。耰，播种后覆土。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">两位农人没有指路，“津”却引出三个人对整条人生道路的不同看法。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-07" name="ly-18-07"></a>

### 18.7　子路从而后

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子路从而后，遇丈人，以杖荷蓧。子路问曰：“子见夫子乎？”丈人曰：“四体不勤，五谷不分，孰为夫子？”植其杖而芸，子路拱而立。止子路宿，杀鸡为黍而食之，见其二子焉。明日，子路行以告，子曰：“隐者也。”使子路反见之，至则行矣。子路曰：“不仕无义。长幼之节不可废也，君臣之义如之何其废之？欲洁其身而乱大伦。君子之仕也，行其义也，道之不行已知之矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子路跟在队伍后面掉了队，问一个扛着农具的老人见过老师没有。老人答，四肢不劳动，五谷分不清，谁知道夫子是谁？他把手杖插好锄草，子路拱手等候。老人留他住宿，杀鸡做黍饭，又叫两个儿子出来见面。第二天孔子听说，认为是隐士，让子路返回拜见，老人已走。子路说，既知道长幼的礼节不可废，君臣之间的责任又怎能全废？君子做官，是要尽其所当为，即使知道道未必行得通。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">蓧，一种农具。芸，通“耘”，除草。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">见儿子、留客吃饭是老人亲自实行的礼；子路由此追问隐居可以舍弃哪些关系。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-08" name="ly-18-08"></a>

### 18.8　逸民

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">逸民：伯夷、叔齐、虞仲、夷逸、朱张、柳下惠、少连。子曰：“不降其志，不辱其身，伯夷、叔齐与！”谓：“柳下惠、少连降志辱身矣，言中伦，行中虑，其斯而已矣。”谓：“虞仲、夷逸隐居放言，身中清，废中权。我则异于是，无可无不可。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">这里列举了伯夷、叔齐、虞仲、夷逸、朱张、柳下惠、少连七位逸民。孔子说，伯夷叔齐没有降低志向、使自身受辱。柳下惠、少连虽曾有所屈就，言语却合乎伦常，行动也经得起思量。虞仲、夷逸隐居自在地说话，立身清白，退处也合乎时宜。至于孔子自己，则没有哪种做法一定要取、一定要弃。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">逸民，隐居或不出仕的人。权，衡量情势而取其宜。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">把三种处境放在一起，再看末句，就知道孔子为何没有为自己挑选一个固定榜样。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-09" name="ly-18-09"></a>

### 18.9　太师挚适齐

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">太师挚适齐，亚饭干适楚，三饭缭适蔡，四饭缺适秦，鼓方叔入于河，播鼗武入于汉，少师阳、击磬襄入于海。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">鲁国的乐官四散：太师挚去了齐国，主持第二、第三、第四次进食奏乐的干、缭、缺分别去了楚、蔡、秦。击鼓的方叔去了黄河一带，摇鼗的武去了汉水一带，少师阳和击磬的襄去了海边。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">亚饭、三饭、四饭，指古代进食时依次奏乐的乐职。鼗，带柄的摇鼓。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">人名、乐职和去处一项项对上，一整套礼乐人员的离散也就呈现在眼前。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-10" name="ly-18-10"></a>

### 18.10　周公谓鲁公曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">周公谓鲁公曰：“君子不施其亲，不使大臣怨乎不以，故旧无大故则不弃也，无求备于一人。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">周公告诫儿子鲁公，不要疏弃亲人，也别让大臣因不获任用而埋怨。旧交若没有重大过失，不要抛下。对一个人，不能要求他样样具备。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">鲁公，指伯禽。不以，这里指不加任用。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“无求备于一人”放在最后，使前三条待人的劝告都有了可以实行的余地。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-18-11" name="ly-18-11"></a>

### 18.11　周有八士

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">周有八士：伯达、伯适、仲突、仲忽、叔夜、叔夏、季随、季騧。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">周代有八位贤士：伯达、伯适、仲突、仲忽、叔夜、叔夏、季随、季騧。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">伯、仲、叔、季，常用来表示兄弟的排行。騧，读 guā。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">本章只存八个名字，读时可以留意四组排行，不必替每个人补写事迹。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-18)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v19" name="ly-v19"></a>

## 子张篇第十九

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-19)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_b18e13b147ec.aspx)

<a id="ly-19-01" name="ly-19-01"></a>

### 19.1　士见危致命

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张曰：“士见危致命，见得思义，祭思敬，丧思哀，其可已矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张说，士人遇见危难肯豁出性命，遇见可得的利益会想到义，祭祀能想到要恭敬，守丧能想到应悲哀，这样就称得上可以了。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">致命，在危难中肯献出性命。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">四种场合各有容易忘记的一件事，连日常的利益取舍也列在其中。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-02" name="ly-19-02"></a>

### 19.2　执德不弘

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张曰：“执德不弘，信道不笃，焉能为有？焉能为亡？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张说，持守德行却胸襟不宽，信奉一种道理却又不坚定，这样的人有他能增添什么，没有他又能减少什么呢？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">弘，宽广。亡，通“无”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">末尾连问“有”和“亡”，衡量的是所持德行在实际上有多大分量。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-03" name="ly-19-03"></a>

### 19.3　子夏之门人问交于子张

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏之门人问交于子张，子张曰：“子夏云何？”对曰：“子夏曰：‘可者与之，其不可者拒之。’”子张曰：“异乎吾所闻。君子尊贤而容众，嘉善而矜不能。我之大贤与，于人何所不容？我之不贤与，人将拒我，如之何其拒人也？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏的学生问怎样交友。子张先问子夏怎么说，听说子夏主张可交者交、不可交者拒，便说自己所学不同。君子尊敬贤者，也能容纳众人，赞赏善行，也怜惜能力不足的人。我若真贤，何人不可容？若我不贤，别人尚且会拒绝我，我又凭什么动辄拒人？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">矜，此处指同情、体谅。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张先复述另一位老师的说法，再把挑选朋友的眼光转到挑选者自己。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-04" name="ly-19-04"></a>

### 19.4　虽小道必有可观者焉

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“虽小道必有可观者焉，致远恐泥，是以君子不为也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，再小的技艺也有值得看的地方。只是若想凭它通到远大之处，恐怕会受阻，所以君子不把它当成终身专务。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">泥，读 nì，拘滞、行不通。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“可观”与“致远”是两个问题，子夏先肯定前者，再说明自己的取舍。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-05" name="ly-19-05"></a>

### 19.5　日知其所亡

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“日知其所亡，月无忘其所能，可谓好学也已矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，每天能知道一点原先不知道的，每月能记住所学会的，这就称得上好学。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">亡，通“无”，指自己原先缺少的知识或本领。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">用“日”看新收获，用“月”看还留下什么，学习有了两种检验方式。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-06" name="ly-19-06"></a>

### 19.6　博学而笃志

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“博学而笃志，切问而近思，仁在其中矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，广泛学习，坚定志向，切实发问，从身边的事想起，仁就在这样的用功当中。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">切问，恳切而切实地发问。近思，就切身的事深入思考。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">先把所学铺开，再把问题放回近处，这几步可以同时进行。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-07" name="ly-19-07"></a>

### 19.7　百工居肆以成其事

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“百工居肆以成其事，君子学以致其道。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，各行工匠在作坊里练成手艺，君子也在学习中走到自己所求的道。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">肆，手工业者工作的场所。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">用工匠的作坊来谈学问，使“学”带上了天天练习的分量。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-08" name="ly-19-08"></a>

### 19.8　小人之过也必文

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“小人之过也必文。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，小人犯了错，总要给它加一层好看的说辞。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">文，在这里作动词，指文饰、掩饰。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一句只写犯错以后做什么，分辨人的关口也就落在事后。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-09" name="ly-19-09"></a>

### 19.9　君子有三变

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“君子有三变：望之俨然，即之也温，听其言也厉。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，从远处看君子，觉得庄重。走到跟前，感到温和。听他开口，又觉得言辞严正。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">俨然，端庄的样子。厉，此处指严正。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">看、接近、听，是观察者靠近的三个步骤，不一定是那个人变了三回。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-10" name="ly-19-10"></a>

### 19.10　君子信而后劳其民

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“君子信而后劳其民，未信，则以为厉己也；信而后谏，未信，则以为谤己也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，君子要先取得百姓信任，再让他们负担劳役，否则人家会以为在折磨自己。对上进谏也是如此，先取得信任再劝谏，否则对方会以为你在毁谤他。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">劳其民，役使百姓做事。厉己，侵害自己。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">前半说对下，后半说对上，两件事都由对方怎样理解你的意图说起。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-11" name="ly-19-11"></a>

### 19.11　大德不逾闲

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“大德不逾闲，小德出入可也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，大的德行不能越过界限，细小的行节则容得有些出入。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">闲，本指栏障，引申为界限。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">难处在于辨别哪一条线关乎大节，哪一处只属细小差异。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-12" name="ly-19-12"></a>

### 19.12　子夏之门人小子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子游曰：“子夏之门人小子，当洒扫应对进退则可矣。抑末也，本之则无，如之何？”子夏闻之，曰：“噫，言游过矣！君子之道，孰先传焉？孰后倦焉？譬诸草木，区以别矣。君子之道焉可诬也？有始有卒者，其惟圣人乎！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子游说，子夏的年轻学生会洒扫、应对、进退，可这些只是枝节，要追问根本却拿不出来，怎么办？子夏说，言游错了。君子之道哪一项该先传、哪一项随后便可怠慢？教人好比区分草木，各有不同。君子之道怎么能任意歪曲？能从开头一直贯彻到末尾的，恐怕只有圣人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">言游，即子游。洒扫应对进退，日常劳动、待人接物的礼节。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">一位老师担心学生停在枝节，另一位提醒别人别轻看初学者手上正在做的事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-13" name="ly-19-13"></a>

### 19.13　仕而优则学

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子夏曰：“仕而优则学，学而优则仕。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子夏说，任职办事而还有余力，就当继续学习。学习有了余力，就可以出来任事。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">优，此处指游刃有余、尚有余力。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">把前半句和后半句合起来看，任职以后仍然有学习的责任。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-14" name="ly-19-14"></a>

### 19.14　丧致乎哀而止

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子游曰：“丧致乎哀而止。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子游说，办理丧事，把哀思表达尽了，就可以止住。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">致，尽其至。乎，相当于“于”。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">“止”给仪节设了一个限度，衡量它的是哀思有没有得到表达。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-15" name="ly-19-15"></a>

### 19.15　吾友张也为难能也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子游曰：“吾友张也为难能也，然而未仁。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子游说，我的朋友子张做得到一些别人难以做到的事，然而还没有达到仁。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">张，子张的字在这里省作一个“张”。子张姓颛孙，名师。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">本领难得是很高的评价，子游仍给“仁”单留了一个问题。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-16" name="ly-19-16"></a>

### 19.16　堂堂乎张也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">曾子曰：“堂堂乎张也，难与并为仁矣。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">曾子说，子张仪表堂堂，可要同他一道修养仁德，却不容易。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">并为仁，一同实践仁。堂堂，形容仪表气度盛大。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">上一章是子游的评语，这一章换成曾子，他说的是与子张相处的感受。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-17" name="ly-19-17"></a>

### 19.17　吾闻诸夫子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">曾子曰：“吾闻诸夫子，人未有自致者也，必也亲丧乎！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">曾子说，我听老师讲，人很少能自发地把真情表达得淋漓尽致，若有这样的场合，大概要数父母之丧。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">自致，自己把心力、情感尽到极处。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">本章不数仪式多少，所问的是哪种感情会不待催促地流露。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-18" name="ly-19-18"></a>

### 19.18　吾闻诸夫子

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">曾子曰：“吾闻诸夫子，孟庄子之孝也，其他可能也；其不改父之臣与父之政，是难能也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">曾子说，我听老师讲，孟庄子的孝，别的地方人家还做得到，难的是他没有随意更换父亲所用的臣子，也没有改掉父亲留下的政事。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">孟庄子，鲁国大夫。臣，此处指家臣。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">提到父亲留下的两样东西，一样是人，一样是已经在进行的事。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-19" name="ly-19-19"></a>

### 19.19　孟氏使阳肤为士师

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孟氏使阳肤为士师，问于曾子。曾子曰：“上失其道，民散久矣。如得其情，则哀矜而勿喜！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孟氏任命阳肤掌刑狱，阳肤来请教曾子。曾子说，上面失了治理之道，百姓离心已久。即使查明犯罪的实情，也该哀怜当事人，别因办成案子而高兴。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">情，案情、实情。哀矜，悲悯、体恤。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">在断案者庆祝查明真相之前，曾子让他回头看百姓长久生活在什么处境里。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-20" name="ly-19-20"></a>

### 19.20　纣之不善

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡曰：“纣之不善，不如是之甚也。是以君子恶居下流，天下之恶皆归焉。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡说，商纣的恶行也不至于后来所说的那么多。因此君子怕自己落在坏名声汇聚之处，一旦在那里，天下种种恶行都会被人记在他头上。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">下流，水流汇聚的低处，这里用来比喻恶名所归。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">承认纣有过恶，同时追问哪些恶行真属于他，这两件事并不矛盾。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-21" name="ly-19-21"></a>

### 19.21　君子之过也

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡曰：“君子之过也，如日月之食焉。过也，人皆见之；更也，人皆仰之。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子贡说，君子的过失像日食、月食，犯错时人人都看得见。等他改过，人人又抬头仰望他。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">更，读 gēng，改正。食，指日食、月食。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">前后两次被人看见，先是缺损，接着是恢复光明，都在众目之下。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-22" name="ly-19-22"></a>

### 19.22　卫公孙朝问于子贡曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">卫公孙朝问于子贡曰：“仲尼焉学？”子贡曰：“文武之道未坠于地，在人。贤者识其大者，不贤者识其小者，莫不有文武之道焉，夫子焉不学？而亦何常师之有？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">卫国的公孙朝问子贡，孔子是跟谁学的？子贡说，文王武王留下的道还在人们身上。贤者记得其中的大处，普通人也记得一些细节，处处都有遗存。老师在哪里不能学，又何必有一个固定的老师？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">识，在这里读 zhì，记住。常师，固定不变的老师。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">回答没有列师承名单，而是说明同一传统怎样分别留在不同人身上。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-23" name="ly-19-23"></a>

### 19.23　叔孙武叔语大夫于朝曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">叔孙武叔语大夫于朝曰：“子贡贤于仲尼。”子服景伯以告子贡，子贡曰：“譬之宫墙，赐之墙也及肩，窥见室家之好；夫子之墙数仞，不得其门而入，不见宗庙之美、百官之富。得其门者或寡矣，夫子之云不亦宜乎！”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">叔孙武叔在朝上说子贡胜过孔子，子服景伯转告子贡。子贡以围墙为喻：我的墙只及肩高，路人就能看到屋舍的好处。老师的墙有好几仞高，不找到门进去，便看不见宗庙的壮美和其中陈设的丰富。找得到门的人很少，武叔会那样说也不奇怪。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">仞，古代长度单位。宫墙，这里指环绕居处的围墙。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">能从墙外看清的，未必是更丰富的一家；可见程度本身会影响评价。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-24" name="ly-19-24"></a>

### 19.24　叔孙武叔毁仲尼

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">叔孙武叔毁仲尼，子贡曰：“无以为也，仲尼不可毁也。他人之贤者，丘陵也，犹可逾也；仲尼，日月也，无得而逾焉。人虽欲自绝，其何伤于日月乎？多见其不知量也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">叔孙武叔诋毁孔子，子贡劝他不必如此。别人的贤德像山丘，还可以越过。孔子却像日月，无法逾越。任凭有人不愿与日月相亲，对日月有何损伤？只越发显出他不知自己的分量。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">自绝，自行断绝关系。多见，越发显出。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">比到日月，批评者能改变的是自己与光明的距离。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-19-25" name="ly-19-25"></a>

### 19.25　陈子禽谓子贡曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">陈子禽谓子贡曰：“子为恭也，仲尼岂贤于子乎？”子贡曰：“君子一言以为知，一言以为不知，言不可不慎也。夫子之不可及也，犹天之不可阶而升也。夫子之得邦家者，所谓立之斯立，道之斯行，绥之斯来，动之斯和。其生也荣，其死也哀，如之何其可及也？”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">陈子禽觉得子贡是客气，问孔子真比他贤明吗？子贡说，一句话能让人看出明智，也能让人看出无知，说话须慎重。老师难以企及，就像天无法靠梯子登上去。倘若老师治理国家或封邑，就能扶持百姓立身、引导他们行进、安抚他们归附、感动他们和睦。他生前受尊崇，去世被哀悼，我哪里比得上？</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">阶，在这里作动词，像登阶梯一样往上走。绥，安抚。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子贡先劝人谨慎下断语，随后把自己心目中的老师放进治理百姓的具体场景里。</font></blockquote>

</div>

[返回本篇章目](#ly-toc-19)　·　[全书逐章目录](#ly-contents)

<a id="ly-v20" name="ly-v20"></a>

## 尧曰篇第二十

[本篇章目](#ly-toc-20)　·　[古文岛本篇原文](https://www.guwendao.net/guwen/bookv_79d87d870a70.aspx)

<a id="ly-20-01" name="ly-20-01"></a>

### 20.1　尧曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">尧曰：“咨！尔舜，天之历数在尔躬，允执其中。四海困穷，天禄永终。”舜亦以命禹。曰：“予小子履，敢用玄牡，敢昭告于皇皇后帝：有罪不敢赦，帝臣不蔽，简在帝心。朕躬有罪，无以万方；万方有罪，罪在朕躬。”周有大赉，善人是富。“虽有周亲，不如仁人。百姓有过，在予一人。”谨权量，审法度，修废官，四方之政行焉。兴灭国，继绝世，举逸民，天下之民归心焉。所重：民、食、丧、祭。宽则得众，信则民任焉，敏则有功，公则说。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">尧告诫舜，承继天下的次序已到你身上，要诚实持守中道。若四海百姓陷入困苦，你的天禄也会断绝。舜把同样的话告诫禹。接着是商汤的告天之辞：我用黑色公牛祭告上帝，有罪者不敢擅赦，有德可用的人不敢遮蔽，一切听凭上帝明察。我自己有罪，不要牵连万方。万方有罪，由我自己承担。周朝广施赏赐，使善人富足。武王看重仁人胜过至亲，也把百姓的过错归责于自己。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">治理天下，还须校正衡器量器、审定法度、恢复废置的官职，使政令通达四方。复兴已灭的国家，接续断绝的世系，起用隐逸的贤人，使人心归附。所重的是百姓、粮食、丧葬和祭祀。待人宽厚能得众，守信使人愿意付托，勤敏可以成事，公正使人心悦。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">履，商汤的名字。玄牡，黑色公牛。赉，赏赐。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">告天的言辞之后写到秤和量器。对君主一人的要求，最后要落实到普通人每天使用的尺度上。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-20-02" name="ly-20-02"></a>

### 20.2　子张问于孔子曰

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">子张问于孔子曰：“何如斯可以从政矣？”子曰：“尊五美，屏四恶，斯可以从政矣。”子张曰：“何谓五美？”子曰：“君子惠而不费，劳而不怨，欲而不贪，泰而不骄，威而不猛。”子张曰：“何谓惠而不费？”子曰：“因民之所利而利之，斯不亦惠而不费乎？择可劳而劳之，又谁怨？欲仁而得仁，又焉贪？君子无众寡，无小大，无敢慢，斯不亦泰而不骄乎？君子正其衣冠，尊其瞻视，俨然人望而畏之，斯不亦威而不猛乎？”子张曰：“何谓四恶？”子曰：“不教而杀谓之虐；不戒视成谓之暴；慢令致期谓之贼；犹之与人也，出纳之吝，谓之有司。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">子张问怎样才可以从政。孔子说，要尊崇五种美德，排除四种恶政。使百姓受惠而不徒费财用，劳民而不招怨，有所求而不贪，安泰而不骄，有威仪而不凶猛，这就是五美。孔子又逐条说明：顺着百姓已有的便利造福他们，便可惠而不费。选确实值得做的事情才动用民力，人便少怨。一心求仁，所求便与贪欲无涉。无论人多人少、地位高低都郑重相待，便能安泰不骄。端正衣冠目光，人看了自然敬畏，便有威而不猛。</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:0;"><font color="#518564">四恶则是：不教就杀，叫虐。事先不告诫，却突然检查成果，叫暴。发令迟缓，却逼人在原定期限内办好，叫贼害。明明决定给人财物，交付时又吝惜计较，便失于办事小吏的狭隘。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">戒，事先告诫、说明。视成，查看结果。有司，本指各有所掌的官吏，这里讥讽当给而吝。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">五美与四恶都写到施政者怎样使用手中权力，尤其可以对照“择可劳”和“慢令致期”。</font></blockquote>

</div>

<a id="ly-20-03" name="ly-20-03"></a>

### 20.3　不知命

<div style="box-sizing:border-box;background-color:#f0efe2;padding:8px 14px;overflow-wrap:anywhere;">


<p title="原文" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;"><font color="#0f0f0f">孔子曰：“不知命，无以为君子也；不知礼，无以立也；不知言，无以知人也。”</font></p>

<p title="白话串讲" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#518564;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#518564">孔子说，不懂得命，就难以成为君子。不懂得礼，就无以立身。听不懂话中的意思，就无从了解说话的人。</font></p>

<p title="词注" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#af9100;margin:6px 0 0;"><font color="#af9100">知言，能辨别言辞的意思与分量。立，指在社会中立身处世。</font></p>

<blockquote title="段评" style="font-family:'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC',sans-serif;font-size:16px;font-weight:400;line-height:2;text-indent:2em;text-align:justify;overflow-wrap:anywhere;color:#0f0f0f;margin:0;background-color:#e9e8dc;border:0;border-radius:2px;margin:12px 0 4px;padding:10px 15px;"><font color="#0f0f0f">全书在“知人”上收尾，可以回看《学而》末章同样提到的“不知人”。</font></blockquote>

</div>
